                            onClick = {
                                val clipboard = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager
                                val clip = ClipData.newPlainText("Game APK Link", PAPERFLOW_APK_URL)
                                clipboard.setPrimaryClip(clip)
                                Toast.makeText(context, "APK link copied to clipboard!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("copy_apk_link_button"),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.ContentCopy,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("Copy APK Link", fontSize = 12.sp)
                        }

                        Button(
                            onClick = {
                                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(PAPERFLOW_WEBSITE_URL))
                                intent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                                try {
                                    context.startActivity(intent)
                                } catch (_: Exception) {
                                    val fallback = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(PAPERFLOW_APK_URL))
                                    fallback.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                                    context.startActivity(fallback)
                                }
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("open_paperflow_button"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = palette.primary),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.OpenInNew,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("Paperflow Web", fontSize = 12.sp)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(30.dp))
            HorizontalDivider(color = palette.border)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(20.dp))

            // 5. Secluded Sign Out Button in Settings Column
            OutlinedButton(
                onClick = { showSignOutConfirmation = true },
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(
                    contentColor = palette.cellErrorText
                ),
                border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, palette.cellErrorText.copy(alpha = 0.6f)),
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(50.dp)
                    .testTag("sign_out_button")
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.Logout,
                    contentDescription = "Sign Out",
                    tint = palette.cellErrorText,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Sign Out of Account",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    fontSize = 15.sp,
                    color = palette.cellErrorText
                )
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
        }
    }

    if (showSignOutConfirmation) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showSignOutConfirmation = false },
            title = {
                Text(
                    text = "Sign Out?",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = palette.cellGivenText
                )
            },
            text = {
                Text(
                    text = "Your active Sudoku sessions, leaderboard ranking, and unlocked stickers will remain safely synced in the cloud. You can sign back in anytime.",
                    color = palette.cellNoteText
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        showSignOutConfirmation = false
                        onDismiss()
                        onSignOut()
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = palette.cellErrorText),
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("confirm_sign_out_button")
                ) {
                    Text("Sign Out", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(
                    onClick = { showSignOutConfirmation = false }
                ) {
                    Text("Cancel", color = palette.cellNoteText)
                }
            },
            containerColor = palette.surfaceElevated
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SectionHeader(
    icon: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.vector.ImageVector,
    title: String,
    palette: com.example.ui.theme.ThemePalette
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = palette.primary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
        )
        Text(
            text = title,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            color = palette.cellGivenText
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ThemeSelectionCard(
    theme: AppTheme,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val palette = LocalThemePalette.current

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
            .background(if (isSelected) palette.primary.copy(alpha = 0.15f) else palette.card)
            .border(
                width = if (isSelected) 1.5.dp else 1.dp,
                color = if (isSelected) palette.primary else palette.border,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
            )
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 10.dp)
            .testTag("theme_option_${theme.id}"),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
    ) {
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            // Theme preview color swatches
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy((-4).dp)) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(24.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(theme.primaryPreviewColor)
                        .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.3f), CircleShape)
                )
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(24.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(theme.accentPreviewColor)
                        .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.3f), CircleShape)
                )
            }

            Column {
                Text(
                    text = theme.displayName,
                    fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    color = if (isSelected) palette.cellGivenText else palette.cellGivenText.copy(alpha = 0.85f)
                )
                Text(
                    text = theme.description,
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                    color = palette.cellNoteText
                )
            }
        }

        if (isSelected) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(22.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(palette.primary),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Check,
                    contentDescription = "Selected",
                    tint = Color.White,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

    companion object {
        fun fromId(id: String): AppTheme {
            return entries.firstOrNull { it.id.equals(id, ignoreCase = true) } ?: OBSIDIAN_VIOLET
        }
    }
}

fun getPaletteForTheme(theme: AppTheme): ThemePalette = when (theme) {
    AppTheme.OBSIDIAN_VIOLET -> ObsidianVioletPalette
    AppTheme.SOLAR_AMBER -> SolarAmberPalette
    AppTheme.CYBER_EMERALD -> CyberEmeraldPalette
    AppTheme.COSMIC_SAPPHIRE -> CosmicSapphirePalette
    AppTheme.CRIMSON_NEON -> CrimsonNeonPalette
}

val LocalThemePalette = staticCompositionLocalOf { ObsidianVioletPalette }
val LocalActiveTheme = staticCompositionLocalOf { AppTheme.OBSIDIAN_VIOLET }

object ThemeStateHolder {
    private val _currentTheme = MutableStateFlow(AppTheme.OBSIDIAN_VIOLET)
    val currentTheme: StateFlow<AppTheme> = _currentTheme.asStateFlow()

    fun initialize(context: Context) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("obsidian_sudoku_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val savedId = prefs.getString("selected_theme_id", AppTheme.OBSIDIAN_VIOLET.id) ?: AppTheme.OBSIDIAN_VIOLET.id
        _currentTheme.value = AppTheme.fromId(savedId)
    }

    fun setTheme(context: Context, theme: AppTheme) {
        _currentTheme.value = theme
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("obsidian_sudoku_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().putString("selected_theme_id", theme.id).apply()
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/ui/theme/Theme.kt
########################################################################################
package com.example.ui.theme

import android.app.Activity
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.CompositionLocalProvider
import androidx.compose.runtime.LaunchedEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.SideEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.collectAsState
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.toArgb
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalView
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat

@Composable
fun ObsidianSudokuTheme(
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        ThemeStateHolder.initialize(context)
    }

    val activeTheme by ThemeStateHolder.currentTheme.collectAsState()
    val palette = getPaletteForTheme(activeTheme)

    val colorScheme = darkColorScheme(
        primary = palette.primary,
        onPrimary = palette.background,
        primaryContainer = palette.surfaceElevated,
        onPrimaryContainer = palette.primaryLight,
        secondary = palette.secondary,
        onSecondary = palette.background,
        secondaryContainer = palette.surfaceElevated,
        onSecondaryContainer = palette.secondaryLight,
        tertiary = palette.tertiary,
        background = palette.background,
        onBackground = palette.cellGivenText,
        surface = palette.surface,
        onSurface = palette.cellGivenText,
        surfaceVariant = palette.card,
        onSurfaceVariant = palette.cellNoteText,
        outline = palette.border,
        error = palette.cellErrorText,
        onError = palette.cellGivenText
    )

    val view = LocalView.current
    if (!view.isInEditMode) {
        SideEffect {
            val window = (view.context as? Activity)?.window
            if (window != null) {
                window.statusBarColor = palette.background.toArgb()
                window.navigationBarColor = palette.background.toArgb()
                WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, view).isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
                WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, view).isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
            }
        }
    }

    CompositionLocalProvider(
        LocalActiveTheme provides activeTheme,
        LocalThemePalette provides palette
    ) {
        MaterialTheme(
            colorScheme = colorScheme,
            typography = Typography,
            content = content
        )
    }
}

import com.example.ui.viewmodel.DailyViewModel
import com.example.ui.viewmodel.DashboardViewModel
import com.example.ui.viewmodel.SocialViewModel
import com.example.ui.viewmodel.StickersViewModel
import com.example.ui.viewmodel.SudokuViewModel
import com.google.firebase.Firebase
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.auth.auth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore

enum class ScreenTab(
    val title: String,
    val selectedIcon: ImageVector,
    val unselectedIcon: ImageVector,
    val testTag: String
) {
    HOME("Home", Icons.Filled.Home, Icons.Outlined.Home, "nav_tab_home"),
    PLAY("Play", Icons.Filled.GridOn, Icons.Outlined.GridOn, "nav_tab_play"),
    DASHBOARD("Dashboard", Icons.Filled.BarChart, Icons.Outlined.BarChart, "nav_tab_dashboard"),
    LEADERBOARD("Rank", Icons.Filled.Leaderboard, Icons.Outlined.Leaderboard, "nav_tab_leaderboard"),
    DAILY("Daily", Icons.Filled.CalendarToday, Icons.Outlined.CalendarToday, "nav_tab_daily")
}


@Composable
fun AppNavigation() {
    var currentUser by remember { mutableStateOf(Firebase.auth.currentUser) }
    var isOfflineGuest by rememberSaveable {
        mutableStateOf(Firebase.auth.currentUser != null)
    }

    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        val listener = FirebaseAuth.AuthStateListener { auth ->
            currentUser = auth.currentUser
            if (auth.currentUser != null) {
                isOfflineGuest = true
            }
        }
        Firebase.auth.addAuthStateListener(listener)
        onDispose {
            Firebase.auth.removeAuthStateListener(listener)
        }
    }

    val user = currentUser
    if (user == null && !isOfflineGuest) {
        AuthScreen(
            onAuthSuccess = {
                currentUser = Firebase.auth.currentUser
                isOfflineGuest = true
            },
            onContinueOffline = {
                isOfflineGuest = true
            }
        )
    } else {
        AuthenticatedAppContent(
            currentUserId = user?.uid ?: "offline_local_player",
            onSignedOut = {
                currentUser = null
                isOfflineGuest = false
            }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun AuthenticatedAppContent(
    currentUserId: String,
    onSignedOut: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val credentialManager = remember(context) { CredentialManager.create(context) }

    val sudokuViewModel: SudokuViewModel = viewModel(
        key = "sudoku_$currentUserId",
        factory = viewModelFactory {
            initializer {
                val app = checkNotNull(this[APPLICATION_KEY])
                val databaseId = app.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
                val db = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(databaseId)
                val dao = SudokuDatabase.getDatabase(app).sudokuDao()
                SudokuViewModel(SudokuRepository(db, dao, app))
            }
        }
    )

    val dailyViewModel: DailyViewModel = viewModel(
        key = "daily_$currentUserId",
        factory = viewModelFactory {
            initializer {
                val app = checkNotNull(this[APPLICATION_KEY])
                val databaseId = app.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
                val db = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(databaseId)
                val dao = SudokuDatabase.getDatabase(app).sudokuDao()
                DailyViewModel(SudokuRepository(db, dao, app))
            }
        }
    )

    val stickersViewModel: StickersViewModel = viewModel(
        key = "stickers_$currentUserId",
        factory = viewModelFactory {
            initializer {
                val app = checkNotNull(this[APPLICATION_KEY])
                val databaseId = app.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
                val db = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(databaseId)
                val dao = SudokuDatabase.getDatabase(app).sudokuDao()
                StickersViewModel(SudokuRepository(db, dao, app))
            }
        }
    )

    val socialViewModel: SocialViewModel = viewModel(
        key = "social_$currentUserId",
        factory = viewModelFactory {
            initializer {
                val app = checkNotNull(this[APPLICATION_KEY])
                val databaseId = app.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
                val db = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(databaseId)
                val dao = SudokuDatabase.getDatabase(app).sudokuDao()
                SocialViewModel(SudokuRepository(db, dao, app))
            }
        }
    )

    val dashboardViewModel: DashboardViewModel = viewModel(
        key = "dashboard_$currentUserId",
        factory = viewModelFactory {
            initializer {
                val app = checkNotNull(this[APPLICATION_KEY])
                val databaseId = app.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
                val db = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(databaseId)
                val dao = SudokuDatabase.getDatabase(app).sudokuDao()
                DashboardViewModel(SudokuRepository(db, dao, app))
            }
        }
    )

    MainScreen(
        sudokuViewModel = sudokuViewModel,
        dailyViewModel = dailyViewModel,
        stickersViewModel = stickersViewModel,
        socialViewModel = socialViewModel,
        dashboardViewModel = dashboardViewModel,
        onSignOut = {
            signOut(
                context = context,
                credentialManager = credentialManager,
                onSignOutComplete = onSignedOut,
                scope = scope
            )
        }
    )
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun MainScreen(
    sudokuViewModel: SudokuViewModel,
    dailyViewModel: DailyViewModel,
    stickersViewModel: StickersViewModel,
    socialViewModel: SocialViewModel,
    dashboardViewModel: DashboardViewModel,
    onSignOut: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    var currentTab by remember { mutableStateOf(ScreenTab.HOME) }
    var showSettingsSheet by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showStickersSheet by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val palette = LocalThemePalette.current
    val socialUiState by socialViewModel.uiState.collectAsState()
    val sudokuUiState by sudokuViewModel.uiState.collectAsState()

    BackHandler(enabled = currentTab != ScreenTab.HOME) {
        currentTab = ScreenTab.HOME
    }

    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        val isWideScreen = maxWidth > 600.dp

        if (isWideScreen) {
            Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                NavigationRail(
                    containerColor = palette.surface,
                    contentColor = palette.cellGivenText,
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("navigation_rail")
                ) {
                    ScreenTab.entries.forEach { tab ->
                        val isSelected = (currentTab == tab)
                        NavigationRailItem(
                            selected = isSelected,
                            onClick = { currentTab = tab },
                            icon = {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = if (isSelected) tab.selectedIcon else tab.unselectedIcon,
                                    contentDescription = tab.title
                                )
                            },
                            label = { Text(tab.title) },
                            colors = NavigationRailItemDefaults.colors(
                                selectedIconColor = palette.primary,
                                selectedTextColor = palette.primary,
                                unselectedIconColor = palette.cellNoteText,
                                unselectedTextColor = palette.cellNoteText,
                                indicatorColor = palette.surfaceElevated
                            ),
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag(tab.testTag)
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = { showSettingsSheet = true },
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("settings_button")
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Settings,
                            contentDescription = "Settings",
                            tint = palette.cellNoteText
                        )
                    }
                }

                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(top = WindowInsets.statusBars.asPaddingValues().calculateTopPadding())
                ) {
                    TopHeaderBar(
                        tab = currentTab,
                        streak = socialUiState.currentStreak,
                        totalScore = sudokuUiState.totalScore,
                        onOpenSettings = { showSettingsSheet = true },
                        palette = palette
                    )
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().weight(1f)) {
                        TabContent(
                            tab = currentTab,
                            sudokuViewModel = sudokuViewModel,
                            dailyViewModel = dailyViewModel,
                            stickersViewModel = stickersViewModel,
                            socialViewModel = socialViewModel,
                            dashboardViewModel = dashboardViewModel,
                            onNavigateToHome = { currentTab = ScreenTab.HOME },
                            onNavigateToPlay = { currentTab = ScreenTab.PLAY },
                            onNavigateToRank = { currentTab = ScreenTab.LEADERBOARD },
                            onNavigateToDashboard = { currentTab = ScreenTab.DASHBOARD },
                            onNavigateToDaily = { currentTab = ScreenTab.DAILY },
                            onNavigateToStickers = { showStickersSheet = true },
                            onStartMatch = { diff ->
                                sudokuViewModel.startNewGame(diff, isDaily = false)
                                currentTab = ScreenTab.PLAY
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            Scaffold(
                containerColor = palette.background,
                topBar = {
                    TopHeaderBar(
                        tab = currentTab,
                        streak = socialUiState.currentStreak,
                        totalScore = sudokuUiState.totalScore,
                        onOpenSettings = { showSettingsSheet = true },
                        palette = palette,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = WindowInsets.statusBars.asPaddingValues().calculateTopPadding())
                    )
                },
                bottomBar = {
                    NavigationBar(
                        containerColor = palette.surface,
                        contentColor = palette.cellGivenText,
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("bottom_nav_bar")
                    ) {
                        ScreenTab.entries.forEach { tab ->
                            val isSelected = (currentTab == tab)
                            NavigationBarItem(
                                selected = isSelected,
                                onClick = { currentTab = tab },
                                icon = {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = if (isSelected) tab.selectedIcon else tab.unselectedIcon,
                                        contentDescription = tab.title
                                    )
                                },
                                label = { Text(tab.title) },
                                colors = NavigationBarItemDefaults.colors(
                                    selectedIconColor = palette.primary,
                                    selectedTextColor = palette.primary,
                                    unselectedIconColor = palette.cellNoteText,
                                    unselectedTextColor = palette.cellNoteText,
                                    indicatorColor = palette.surfaceElevated
                                ),
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag(tab.testTag)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            ) { innerPadding ->
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(innerPadding)
                ) {
                    TabContent(
                        tab = currentTab,
                        sudokuViewModel = sudokuViewModel,
                        dailyViewModel = dailyViewModel,
                        stickersViewModel = stickersViewModel,
                        socialViewModel = socialViewModel,
                        dashboardViewModel = dashboardViewModel,
                        onNavigateToHome = { currentTab = ScreenTab.HOME },
                        onNavigateToPlay = { currentTab = ScreenTab.PLAY },
                        onNavigateToRank = { currentTab = ScreenTab.LEADERBOARD },
                        onNavigateToDashboard = { currentTab = ScreenTab.DASHBOARD },
                        onNavigateToDaily = { currentTab = ScreenTab.DAILY },
                        onNavigateToStickers = { showStickersSheet = true },
                        onStartMatch = { diff ->
                            sudokuViewModel.startNewGame(diff, isDaily = false)
                            currentTab = ScreenTab.PLAY
                        }
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showSettingsSheet) {
        SettingsColumnSheet(
            socialViewModel = socialViewModel,
            onDismiss = { showSettingsSheet = false },
            onSignOut = onSignOut
        )
    }

    if (showStickersSheet) {
        val sheetState = rememberModalBottomSheetState(skipPartiallyExpanded = true)
        ModalBottomSheet(
            onDismissRequest = { showStickersSheet = false },
            sheetState = sheetState,
            containerColor = palette.surface,
            contentColor = palette.cellGivenText
        ) {
            Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                StickersScreen(viewModel = stickersViewModel)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun TopHeaderBar(
    tab: ScreenTab,
    streak: Int,
    totalScore: Int,
    onOpenSettings: () -> Unit,
    palette: com.example.ui.theme.ThemePalette,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            Text(
                text = "Obsidian Sudoku",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                color = palette.cellGivenText
            )

            // Live streak badge: shows streak for solving ANY puzzle!
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .background(palette.secondary.copy(alpha = 0.18f))
                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                    .testTag("header_streak_badge")
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "🔥 $streak",
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = palette.secondary
                )
            }

            // Live Rating Score badge
            if (totalScore > 0) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(WarmAmber.copy(alpha = 0.18f))
                        .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                        .testTag("header_score_badge")
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "⭐ $totalScore",
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = WarmAmber
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Secluded Settings in top corner
        IconButton(
            onClick = onOpenSettings,
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(40.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                .background(palette.card)
                .testTag("settings_button")
        ) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = Icons.Default.Settings,
                contentDescription = "Settings",
                tint = palette.primary,
                modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun TabContent(
    tab: ScreenTab,
    sudokuViewModel: SudokuViewModel,
    dailyViewModel: DailyViewModel,
    stickersViewModel: StickersViewModel,
    socialViewModel: SocialViewModel,
    dashboardViewModel: DashboardViewModel,
    onNavigateToHome: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToPlay: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToRank: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToDashboard: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToDaily: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToStickers: () -> Unit,
    onStartMatch: (Difficulty) -> Unit
) {
    when (tab) {
        ScreenTab.HOME -> HomeScreen(
            sudokuViewModel = sudokuViewModel,
            socialViewModel = socialViewModel,
            onStartMatch = onStartMatch,
            onNavigateToRank = onNavigateToRank,
            onNavigateToDashboard = onNavigateToDashboard,
            onNavigateToDaily = onNavigateToDaily,
            onNavigateToStickers = onNavigateToStickers
        )
        ScreenTab.PLAY -> PlayScreen(
            viewModel = sudokuViewModel,
            onGoHome = onNavigateToHome
        )
        ScreenTab.DASHBOARD -> DashboardScreen(
            viewModel = dashboardViewModel,
            onNavigateToPlay = onNavigateToPlay
        )
        ScreenTab.LEADERBOARD -> LeaderboardScreen(viewModel = socialViewModel)
        ScreenTab.DAILY -> DailyScreen(
            viewModel = dailyViewModel,
            onStartDailyGame = {
                sudokuViewModel.startNewGame(Difficulty.DAILY, isDaily = true)
                onNavigateToPlay()
            }
        )
    }
}

package com.example.ui.viewmodel

import android.util.Log
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.example.data.model.StickerEntity
import com.example.data.repository.SudokuRepository
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.catch
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn

data class StickersUiState(
    val stickers: List<StickerEntity> = emptyList(),
    val unlockedCount: Int = 0,
    val totalCount: Int = 0,
    val errorMessage: String? = null
)

class StickersViewModel(
    repository: SudokuRepository
) : ViewModel() {

    val uiState: StateFlow<StickersUiState> = repository.stickers
        .map { list ->
            val displayList = list.ifEmpty { SudokuRepository.defaultStickerCatalog() }
            StickersUiState(
                stickers = displayList,
                unlockedCount = displayList.count { it.unlocked },
                totalCount = displayList.size
            )
        }
        .catch { error ->
            Log.w("StickersViewModel", "Error observing stickers", error)
            val fallback = SudokuRepository.defaultStickerCatalog()
            emit(
                StickersUiState(
                    stickers = fallback,
                    unlockedCount = 0,
                    totalCount = fallback.size,
                    errorMessage = error.localizedMessage
                )
            )
        }
        .stateIn(
            viewModelScope,
            SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
            StickersUiState(
                stickers = SudokuRepository.defaultStickerCatalog(),
                totalCount = SudokuRepository.defaultStickerCatalog().size
            )
        )
}

package com.example.ui.viewmodel

import android.util.Log
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.example.data.model.DailyChallengeEntity
import com.example.data.repository.SudokuRepository
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.catch
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn

data class DailyUiState(
    val todayDate: String,
    val streak: Int = 0,
    val isTodayCompleted: Boolean = false,
    val todayChallenge: DailyChallengeEntity? = null,
    val recentChallenges: List<DailyChallengeEntity> = emptyList(),
    val errorMessage: String? = null
)

class DailyViewModel(
    private val repository: SudokuRepository
) : ViewModel() {

    val todayDate: String = repository.getTodayDateString()

    val uiState: StateFlow<DailyUiState> = repository.allDailyChallenges
        .map { list ->
            val today = list.firstOrNull { it.dateString == todayDate }
            val streak = repository.calculateStreak(list)
            DailyUiState(
                todayDate = todayDate,
                streak = streak,
                isTodayCompleted = today?.completed == true,
                todayChallenge = today,
                recentChallenges = list.take(14)
            )
        }
        .catch { error ->
            Log.w("DailyViewModel", "Error observing daily challenges", error)
            emit(DailyUiState(todayDate = todayDate, errorMessage = error.localizedMessage ?: "Failed to load daily challenges"))
        }
        .stateIn(
            viewModelScope,
            SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
            DailyUiState(todayDate = todayDate)
        )
}


########################################################################################
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/ui/viewmodel/SudokuViewModel.kt
########################################################################################
package com.example.ui.viewmodel

import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.example.data.model.GameSessionEntity
import com.example.data.repository.SudokuRepository
import com.example.logic.Difficulty
import com.example.logic.ScoreResult
import com.example.logic.SudokuEngine
import com.example.ui.model.Cell
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.firstOrNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class BoardSnapshot(
    val board: List<List<Cell>>,
    val mistakes: Int
)

data class SudokuUiState(
    val board: List<List<Cell>> = createEmptyBoard(),
    val solution: Array<IntArray> = Array(9) { IntArray(9) },
    val selectedRow: Int? = null,
    val selectedCol: Int? = null,
    val difficulty: Difficulty = Difficulty.EASY,
    val isDaily: Boolean = false,
    val timerSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val isPaused: Boolean = false,
    val mistakes: Int = 0,
    val maxMistakes: Int = 5,
    val isGameOver: Boolean = false,
    val isGameWon: Boolean = false,
    val isNotesMode: Boolean = false,
    val remainingCounts: Map<Int, Int> = (1..9).associateWith { 9 },
    val feedbackMessage: String? = null,
    val lastMatchScore: ScoreResult? = null,
    val totalScore: Int = 0
)


fun createEmptyBoard(): List<List<Cell>> {
    return List(9) { r ->
        List(9) { c ->
            Cell(row = r, col = c)
        }
    }
}

class SudokuViewModel(
    private val repository: SudokuRepository
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(SudokuUiState())
    val uiState: StateFlow<SudokuUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()

    private val undoStack = mutableListOf<BoardSnapshot>()
    private var timerJob: Job? = null

    init {
        populateInitialBoardSynchronously(Difficulty.EASY)
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val session = repository.getActiveSessionOnce()
                if (session != null && session.boardData.isNotEmpty()) {
                    restoreSession(session)
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Keep initial generated board
            }
        }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.initDefaultStickers()
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Handled by repository error logger
            }
        }
        startTimerLoop()
    }

    private fun populateInitialBoardSynchronously(difficulty: Difficulty) {
        val generated = SudokuEngine.generatePuzzle(difficulty, System.currentTimeMillis())
        val newBoard = List(9) { r ->
            List(9) { c ->
                val v = generated.initialBoard[r][c]
                Cell(
                    row = r,
                    col = c,
                    value = v,
                    isGiven = (v != 0),
                    notes = emptySet()
                )
            }
        }
        _uiState.value = SudokuUiState(
            board = newBoard,
            solution = generated.solution,
            difficulty = difficulty,
            maxMistakes = difficulty.maxMistakes,
            remainingCounts = calculateRemainingCounts(newBoard),
            feedbackMessage = "${difficulty.displayName} Game Started",
            totalScore = repository.getUserTotalScore()
        )
    }


    private fun startTimerLoop() {
        timerJob?.cancel()
        timerJob = viewModelScope.launch {
            var saveCounter = 0
            while (true) {
                delay(1000)
                val state = _uiState.value
                if (!state.isPaused && !state.isGameOver && !state.isGameWon) {
                    _uiState.update { it.copy(timerSeconds = it.timerSeconds + 1) }
                    saveCounter++
                    if (saveCounter >= 5) {
                        saveCounter = 0
                        persistCurrentSession()
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun persistCurrentSession() {
        val s = _uiState.value
        if (s.isGameOver || s.isGameWon) return
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val boardStr = serializeBoard(s.board)
                val solutionStr = serializeIntMatrix(s.solution)
                val notesStr = serializeNotes(s.board)
                repository.saveActiveSession(
                    difficulty = s.difficulty.name,
                    timerSeconds = s.timerSeconds,
                    mistakes = s.mistakes,
                    isPaused = s.isPaused,
                    isDaily = s.isDaily,
                    boardData = boardStr,
                    solutionData = solutionStr,
                    notesData = notesStr
                )
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Handled by structured FirestoreErrorHandler in repository
            }
        }
    }

    fun startNewGame(difficulty: Difficulty, isDaily: Boolean = false) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.clearActiveSession()
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Ignore if no active session exists
            }
            val seed = if (isDaily) repository.getTodayDateString().hashCode().toLong() else System.currentTimeMillis()
            val generated = SudokuEngine.generatePuzzle(difficulty, seed)

            val newBoard = List(9) { r ->
                List(9) { c ->
                    val v = generated.initialBoard[r][c]
                    Cell(
                        row = r,
                        col = c,
                        value = v,
                        isGiven = (v != 0),
                        notes = emptySet()
                    )
                }
            }

            undoStack.clear()
            _uiState.update {
                it.copy(
                    board = newBoard,
                    solution = generated.solution,
                    selectedRow = null,
                    selectedCol = null,
                    difficulty = difficulty,
                    isDaily = isDaily,
                    timerSeconds = 0L,
                    isPaused = false,
                    mistakes = 0,
                    maxMistakes = difficulty.maxMistakes,
                    isGameOver = false,
                    isGameWon = false,
                    isNotesMode = false,
                    remainingCounts = calculateRemainingCounts(newBoard),
                    feedbackMessage = if (isDaily) "Daily Challenge Initialized!" else "${difficulty.displayName} Game Started",
                    lastMatchScore = null,
                    totalScore = repository.getUserTotalScore()
                )
            }
            persistCurrentSession()
        }
    }

    fun onCellClick(row: Int, col: Int) {
        if (_uiState.value.isPaused || _uiState.value.isGameOver || _uiState.value.isGameWon) return
        _uiState.update {
            it.copy(
                selectedRow = if (it.selectedRow == row && it.selectedCol == col) null else row,
                selectedCol = if (it.selectedRow == row && it.selectedCol == col) null else col,
                feedbackMessage = null
            )
        }
    }

    fun onNumberInput(digit: Int) {
        val state = _uiState.value
        val r = state.selectedRow ?: return
        val c = state.selectedCol ?: return
        if (state.isPaused || state.isGameOver || state.isGameWon) return

        val currentCell = state.board[r][c]
        if (currentCell.isGiven) return

        if (state.isNotesMode) {
            // Toggle note
            val newNotes = if (currentCell.notes.contains(digit)) {
                currentCell.notes - digit
            } else {
                currentCell.notes + digit
            }
            updateCell(r, c) { it.copy(notes = newNotes, value = 0, isError = false) }
            return
        }

        // Normal number input
        saveUndoSnapshot()

        // Move validation against current board & solution
        val currentMatrix = Array(9) { rowIdx -> IntArray(9) { colIdx -> state.board[rowIdx][colIdx].value } }
        val valResult = SudokuEngine.validateMove(currentMatrix, state.solution, r, c, digit)

        if (!valResult.isCorrectAgainstSolution) {
            // Mistake
            val newMistakes = state.mistakes + 1
            val isOver = newMistakes >= state.maxMistakes
            updateCell(r, c) { it.copy(value = digit, isError = true, notes = emptySet()) }
            _uiState.update {
                it.copy(
                    mistakes = newMistakes,
                    isGameOver = isOver,
                    feedbackMessage = if (isOver) "Game Over! Too many mistakes." else "Incorrect placement! ($newMistakes/${state.maxMistakes})"
                )
            }
            if (isOver) {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    try {
                        val scoreRes = repository.recordGameFinished(state.difficulty.name, state.timerSeconds, newMistakes, isWon = false)
                        repository.clearActiveSession()
                        _uiState.update {
                            it.copy(
                                lastMatchScore = scoreRes,
                                totalScore = repository.getUserTotalScore()
                            )
                        }
                    } catch (_: Exception) {
                        // Logged in repository
                    }
                }
            }
            return
        }

        // Correct digit
        val updatedBoard = updateCellAndGetBoard(r, c) {
            it.copy(value = digit, isError = false, notes = emptySet())
        }
        // Remove this digit from notes in same row, column, and 3x3 box
        val cleanedBoard = cleanNotes(updatedBoard, r, c, digit)
        val remaining = calculateRemainingCounts(cleanedBoard)

        // Check if game won
        val isWon = isBoardFullySolved(cleanedBoard, state.solution)
        _uiState.update {
            it.copy(
                board = cleanedBoard,
                remainingCounts = remaining,
                isGameWon = isWon,
                feedbackMessage = if (isWon) "Victory! Puzzle Solved!" else null
            )
        }

        if (isWon) {
            handleVictory()
        } else {
            persistCurrentSession()
        }
    }

    private fun handleVictory() {
        val s = _uiState.value
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val scoreRes = repository.recordGameFinished(s.difficulty.name, s.timerSeconds, s.mistakes, isWon = true)
                if (s.isDaily) {
                    repository.completeDailyChallenge(
                        dateString = repository.getTodayDateString(),
                        difficulty = s.difficulty.name,
                        timeSeconds = s.timerSeconds,
                        mistakes = s.mistakes,
                        stars = s.difficulty.stars
                    )
                }
                repository.clearActiveSession()
                _uiState.update {
                    it.copy(
                        lastMatchScore = scoreRes,
                        totalScore = repository.getUserTotalScore()
                    )
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Logged in repository
            }
        }
    }


    fun onErase() {
        val state = _uiState.value
        val r = state.selectedRow ?: return
        val c = state.selectedCol ?: return
        if (state.isPaused || state.isGameOver || state.isGameWon) return

        val cell = state.board[r][c]
        if (cell.isGiven) return

        saveUndoSnapshot()
        val updated = updateCellAndGetBoard(r, c) {
            it.copy(value = 0, isError = false, notes = emptySet())
        }
        _uiState.update {
            it.copy(
                board = updated,
                remainingCounts = calculateRemainingCounts(updated)
            )
        }
        persistCurrentSession()
    }

    fun onNotesToggle() {
        _uiState.update { it.copy(isNotesMode = !it.isNotesMode) }
    }

    fun onHint() {
        val state = _uiState.value
        if (state.isPaused || state.isGameOver || state.isGameWon) return

        val currentMatrix = Array(9) { r -> IntArray(9) { c -> state.board[r][c].value } }
        val hint = SudokuEngine.findHint(currentMatrix, state.solution) ?: return

        saveUndoSnapshot()
        val updatedBoard = updateCellAndGetBoard(hint.row, hint.col) {
            it.copy(
                value = hint.value,
                isHint = true,
                isError = false,
                notes = emptySet()
            )
        }
        val cleaned = cleanNotes(updatedBoard, hint.row, hint.col, hint.value)
        val remaining = calculateRemainingCounts(cleaned)
        val isWon = isBoardFullySolved(cleaned, state.solution)

        _uiState.update {
            it.copy(
                board = cleaned,
                selectedRow = hint.row,
                selectedCol = hint.col,
                remainingCounts = remaining,
                isGameWon = isWon,
                feedbackMessage = "Hint placed at (${hint.row + 1}, ${hint.col + 1})"
            )
        }

        if (isWon) {
            handleVictory()
        } else {
            persistCurrentSession()
        }
    }

    fun onUndo() {
        if (undoStack.isEmpty()) return
        val snapshot = undoStack.removeAt(undoStack.lastIndex)
        _uiState.update {
            it.copy(
                board = snapshot.board,
                mistakes = snapshot.mistakes,
                remainingCounts = calculateRemainingCounts(snapshot.board),
                feedbackMessage = "Move undone"
            )
        }
        persistCurrentSession()
    }

    fun onTogglePause() {
        val newPaused = !_uiState.value.isPaused
        _uiState.update { it.copy(isPaused = newPaused) }
        persistCurrentSession()
    }

    fun resumeGame() {
        _uiState.update { it.copy(isPaused = false) }
        persistCurrentSession()
    }

    fun resetBoard() {
        val s = _uiState.value
        val resetBoard = s.board.map { row ->
            row.map { cell ->
                if (cell.isGiven) cell
                else cell.copy(value = 0, isError = false, isHint = false, notes = emptySet())
            }
        }
        undoStack.clear()
        _uiState.update {
            it.copy(
                board = resetBoard,
                mistakes = 0,
                isGameOver = false,
                isGameWon = false,
                remainingCounts = calculateRemainingCounts(resetBoard),
                feedbackMessage = "Board reset to initial clues"
            )
        }
        persistCurrentSession()
    }

    val canUndo: Boolean
        get() = undoStack.isNotEmpty()

    private fun saveUndoSnapshot() {
        val s = _uiState.value
        undoStack.add(BoardSnapshot(board = s.board, mistakes = s.mistakes))
        if (undoStack.size > 20) {
            undoStack.removeAt(0)
        }
    }

    private fun updateCell(r: Int, c: Int, transform: (Cell) -> Cell) {
        val newBoard = updateCellAndGetBoard(r, c, transform)
        _uiState.update { it.copy(board = newBoard) }
    }

    private fun updateCellAndGetBoard(r: Int, c: Int, transform: (Cell) -> Cell): List<List<Cell>> {
        return _uiState.value.board.mapIndexed { rowIdx, row ->
            if (rowIdx == r) {
                row.mapIndexed { colIdx, cell ->
                    if (colIdx == c) transform(cell) else cell
                }
            } else row
        }
    }

    private fun cleanNotes(board: List<List<Cell>>, row: Int, col: Int, digit: Int): List<List<Cell>> {
        val boxR = (row / 3) * 3
        val boxC = (col / 3) * 3
        return board.mapIndexed { r, rowList ->
            rowList.mapIndexed { c, cell ->
                if (r == row || c == col || (r in boxR until boxR + 3 && c in boxC until boxC + 3)) {
                    if (cell.notes.contains(digit)) {
                        cell.copy(notes = cell.notes - digit)
                    } else cell
                } else cell
            }
        }
    }

    private fun calculateRemainingCounts(board: List<List<Cell>>): Map<Int, Int> {
        val counts = mutableMapOf<Int, Int>()
        for (i in 1..9) counts[i] = 9
        for (row in board) {
            for (cell in row) {
                if (cell.value in 1..9 && !cell.isError) {
                    val cur = counts[cell.value] ?: 9
                    counts[cell.value] = (cur - 1).coerceAtLeast(0)
                }
            }
        }
        return counts
    }

    private fun isBoardFullySolved(board: List<List<Cell>>, solution: Array<IntArray>): Boolean {
        val matrix = Array(9) { r -> IntArray(9) { c -> board[r][c].value } }
        return SudokuEngine.checkWinCondition(matrix, solution).isWon
    }


    private fun serializeBoard(board: List<List<Cell>>): String {
        return board.flatten().joinToString(",") { "${it.value}:${if (it.isGiven) 1 else 0}:${if (it.isError) 1 else 0}" }
    }

    private fun serializeIntMatrix(matrix: Array<IntArray>): String {
        return matrix.flatMap { it.asIterable() }.joinToString(",")
    }

    private fun serializeNotes(board: List<List<Cell>>): String {
        return board.flatten().joinToString(";") { it.notes.joinToString(",") }
    }

    private fun restoreSession(session: GameSessionEntity) {
        try {
            val cellItems = session.boardData.split(",")
            val solutionDigits = session.solutionData.split(",").map { it.toInt() }
            val noteItems = session.notesData.split(";")

            val solMatrix = Array(9) { r ->
                IntArray(9) { c ->
                    solutionDigits[r * 9 + c]
                }
            }

            val restoredBoard = List(9) { r ->
                List(9) { c ->
                    val idx = r * 9 + c
                    val parts = cellItems[idx].split(":")
                    val v = parts[0].toInt()
                    val isGiven = parts.getOrNull(1) == "1"
                    val isError = parts.getOrNull(2) == "1"
                    val notesSet = noteItems.getOrNull(idx)
                        ?.split(",")
                        ?.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
                        ?.mapNotNull { it.toIntOrNull() }
                        ?.toSet() ?: emptySet()

                    Cell(
                        row = r,
                        col = c,
                        value = v,
                        isGiven = isGiven,
                        isError = isError,
                        notes = notesSet
                    )
                }
            }

            val diff = Difficulty.fromName(session.difficulty)
            _uiState.update {
                it.copy(
                    board = restoredBoard,
                    solution = solMatrix,
                    difficulty = diff,
                    isDaily = session.isDaily,
                    timerSeconds = session.timerSeconds,
                    mistakes = session.mistakes,
                    maxMistakes = diff.maxMistakes,
                    isPaused = session.isPaused,
                    remainingCounts = calculateRemainingCounts(restoredBoard),
                    feedbackMessage = "Game resumed"
                )
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            startNewGame(Difficulty.EASY, isDaily = false)
        }
    }
}

########################################################################################
package com.example.ui.viewmodel

import android.util.Log
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.example.data.model.GameStatsEntity
import com.example.data.repository.SudokuRepository
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.catch
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class LeaderboardEntry(
    val rank: Int,
    val name: String,
    val title: String,
    val score: Int,
    val bestTime: String,
    val isCurrentUser: Boolean = false
)

data class SocialUiState(
    val statsList: List<GameStatsEntity> = emptyList(),
    val leaderboard: List<LeaderboardEntry> = emptyList(),
    val totalGamesWon: Int = 0,
    val totalTimeFormatted: String = "00:00",
    val currentStreak: Int = 0,
    val publicUsername: String = "Player",
    val errorMessage: String? = null
)

class SocialViewModel(
    private val repository: SudokuRepository
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _usernameState = MutableStateFlow(repository.getPublicUsername())
    val usernameState: StateFlow<String> = _usernameState.asStateFlow()

    val uiState: StateFlow<SocialUiState> = combine(
        repository.allStats,
        repository.observeLeaderboard(),
        _usernameState
    ) { stats, leaderboard, currentUsername ->
        val totalWon = stats.sumOf { it.gamesWon }
        val totalSecs = stats.sumOf { it.totalTimeSeconds }
        val hours = totalSecs / 3600
        val mins = (totalSecs % 3600) / 60
        val timeStr = if (hours > 0) "${hours}h ${mins}m" else "${mins}m ${totalSecs % 60}s"
        val streak = repository.getOverallStreak()

        SocialUiState(
            statsList = stats,
            leaderboard = leaderboard,
            totalGamesWon = totalWon,
            totalTimeFormatted = timeStr,
            currentStreak = streak,
            publicUsername = currentUsername
        )
    }.catch { error ->
        Log.w("SocialViewModel", "Error observing realtime leaderboard or stats", error)
        emit(
            SocialUiState(
                publicUsername = repository.getPublicUsername(),
                errorMessage = error.localizedMessage
            )
        )
    }.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        SocialUiState(
            publicUsername = repository.getPublicUsername(),
            currentStreak = repository.getOverallStreak()
        )
    )

    fun updatePublicUsername(newUsername: String) {
        val trimmed = newUsername.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return
        _usernameState.value = trimmed
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                repository.setPublicUsername(trimmed)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w("SocialViewModel", "Failed to update public username", e)
            }
        }
    }
}


########################################################################################
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/ui/viewmodel/DashboardViewModel.kt
########################################################################################
package com.example.ui.viewmodel

import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.example.data.model.GameStatsEntity
import com.example.data.model.SolvedPuzzleEntity
import com.example.data.repository.SudokuRepository
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import java.util.Locale

data class DashboardUiState(
    val totalSolved: Int = 0,
    val totalPlayed: Int = 0,
    val totalScore: Int = 0,
    val winRatePercent: Float = 0f,
    val bestTimeFormatted: String = "--:--",
    val currentStreak: Int = 0,
    val flawlessCount: Int = 0,
    val statsList: List<GameStatsEntity> = emptyList(),
    val allSolvedPuzzles: List<SolvedPuzzleEntity> = emptyList(),
    val filteredSolvedPuzzles: List<SolvedPuzzleEntity> = emptyList(),
    val selectedFilter: String = "ALL"
)

class DashboardViewModel(
    private val repository: SudokuRepository
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _selectedFilter = MutableStateFlow("ALL")
    val selectedFilter: StateFlow<String> = _selectedFilter.asStateFlow()

    val uiState: StateFlow<DashboardUiState> = combine(
        repository.allStats,
        repository.allSolvedPuzzles,
        repository.userTotalScoreFlow,
        _selectedFilter
    ) { stats, solvedPuzzles, totalScore, filter ->
        val totalPlayed = stats.sumOf { it.gamesPlayed }
        val totalWon = maxOf(stats.sumOf { it.gamesWon }, solvedPuzzles.size)
        val flawless = maxOf(stats.sumOf { it.perfectGames }, solvedPuzzles.count { it.mistakes == 0 })
        val winRate = if (totalPlayed > 0) (totalWon.toFloat() / totalPlayed * 100f).coerceAtMost(100f) else (if (totalWon > 0) 100f else 0f)

        val bestSecs = solvedPuzzles.minOfOrNull { it.timeSpentSeconds }
            ?: stats.filter { it.bestTimeSeconds > 0 }.minOfOrNull { it.bestTimeSeconds }
            ?: 0L

        val bestTimeStr = if (bestSecs > 0) {
            val mins = bestSecs / 60
            val secs = bestSecs % 60
            String.format(Locale.US, "%02d:%02d", mins, secs)
        } else {
            "--:--"
        }

        val filtered = when (filter.uppercase(Locale.US)) {
            "ALL" -> solvedPuzzles
            "FLAWLESS" -> solvedPuzzles.filter { it.mistakes == 0 }
            "DAILY" -> solvedPuzzles.filter { it.isDaily }
            else -> solvedPuzzles.filter { it.difficulty.equals(filter, ignoreCase = true) }
        }

        DashboardUiState(
            totalSolved = totalWon,
            totalPlayed = maxOf(totalPlayed, totalWon),
            totalScore = totalScore,
            winRatePercent = winRate,
            bestTimeFormatted = bestTimeStr,
            currentStreak = repository.getOverallStreak(),
            flawlessCount = flawless,
            statsList = stats,
            allSolvedPuzzles = solvedPuzzles,
            filteredSolvedPuzzles = filtered,
            selectedFilter = filter
        )
    }.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        DashboardUiState(
            totalScore = repository.getUserTotalScore(),
            currentStreak = repository.getOverallStreak()
        )
    )

    fun setFilter(filter: String) {
        _selectedFilter.value = filter
    }
}

                LeaderboardRow(entry = entry)
            }
        }

@Composable
fun LeaderboardRow(entry: LeaderboardEntry) {
    val palette = LocalThemePalette.current
    val isUser = entry.isCurrentUser
    val medalColor = when (entry.rank) {
        1 -> Color(0xFFFFD700) // Gold
        2 -> Color(0xFFC0C0C0) // Silver
        3 -> Color(0xFFCD7F32) // Bronze
        else -> palette.cellNoteText
    }

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(if (isUser) palette.primary.copy(alpha = 0.18f) else palette.card)
            .border(
                1.dp,
                if (isUser) palette.primary else palette.border,
                RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp)
            .testTag(if (isUser) "current_user_rank_row" else "leaderboard_rank_${entry.rank}"),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
    ) {
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
        ) {
            // Rank Badge
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(32.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(
                        if (entry.rank <= 3) medalColor.copy(alpha = 0.2f)
                        else palette.surface
                    ),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "#${entry.rank}",
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = if (entry.rank <= 3) medalColor else palette.cellNoteText
                )
            }

            Column {
                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = entry.name,
                        fontWeight = if (isUser) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        color = if (isUser) palette.secondary else palette.cellGivenText,
                        fontSize = 15.sp
                    )
                    if (isUser) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                .background(palette.primary.copy(alpha = 0.25f))
                                .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "YOU",
                                fontSize = 9.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = palette.primary
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
                Text(
                    text = entry.title,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = palette.cellNoteText
                )
            }
        }

        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End) {
            Text(
                text = "${entry.score} pts",
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                color = if (isUser) palette.secondary else palette.cellGivenText
            )
            Text(
                text = "Best: ${entry.bestTime}",
                fontSize = 11.sp,
                color = palette.cellNoteText
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun StickersScreen(
    viewModel: StickersViewModel,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsState()

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        // Unlocked Progress Banner
        Card(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .testTag("stickers_progress_banner"),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = ObsidianSurface),
            border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, ObsidianViolet.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(20.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Column {
                    Text(
                        text = "OBSIDIAN RELICS",
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = NeonCyan,
                        letterSpacing = 1.sp
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "${uiState.unlockedCount} / ${uiState.totalCount} Unlocked",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = Color.White
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = "Earn badges by mastering speed, precision, and streaks",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = Color.Gray
                    )
                }

                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(54.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(ObsidianViolet.copy(alpha = 0.2f)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Diamond,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = ObsidianViolet,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(30.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Stickers 2-Column Grid
        LazyVerticalGrid(
            columns = GridCells.Fixed(2),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .testTag("stickers_grid")
        ) {
            items(uiState.stickers, key = { it.id }) { sticker ->
                StickerCard(sticker = sticker)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun StickerCard(sticker: StickerEntity) {
    val isUnlocked = sticker.unlocked
    val iconVector = getIconForSticker(sticker.iconName)

    Card(
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
            containerColor = if (isUnlocked) ObsidianCard else ObsidianSurface.copy(alpha = 0.5f)
        ),
        border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(
            1.dp,
            if (isUnlocked) ObsidianBorderStrong else ObsidianBorder.copy(alpha = 0.4f)
        ),
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .testTag("sticker_card_${sticker.id}")
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(16.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(56.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(
                        if (isUnlocked) ObsidianPrimary.copy(alpha = 0.2f)
                        else Color.DarkGray.copy(alpha = 0.3f)
                    )
                    .border(
                        1.dp,
                        if (isUnlocked) NeonCyan else Color.Transparent,
                        CircleShape
                    ),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                if (isUnlocked) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = iconVector,
                        contentDescription = sticker.title,
                        tint = if (sticker.category == "Streaks") WarmAmber else ObsidianViolet,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                    )
                } else {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Lock,
                        contentDescription = "Locked",
                        tint = Color.Gray,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                    )
                }
            }

            Text(
                text = sticker.title,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                color = if (isUnlocked) Color.White else Color.Gray,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )

            Text(
                text = sticker.description,
                fontSize = 11.sp,
                color = if (isUnlocked) Color.LightGray else Color.DarkGray,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                lineHeight = 15.sp
            )

            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                    .background(
                        if (isUnlocked) EmeraldGreen.copy(alpha = 0.15f)
                        else Color.Transparent
                    )
                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = if (isUnlocked) "UNLOCKED" else "LOCKED",
                    fontSize = 9.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = if (isUnlocked) EmeraldGreen else Color.Gray,
                    letterSpacing = 0.5.sp
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

fun getIconForSticker(iconName: String): ImageVector {
    return when (iconName) {
        "sparkles" -> Icons.Default.AutoAwesome
        "bolt" -> Icons.Default.Bolt
        "diamond" -> Icons.Default.Diamond
        "military_tech" -> Icons.Default.MilitaryTech
        "local_fire_department" -> Icons.Default.LocalFireDepartment
        "brightness_auto" -> Icons.Default.BrightnessAuto
        "edit_note" -> Icons.Default.EditNote
        "auto_awesome" -> Icons.Default.AutoAwesome
        else -> Icons.Default.Diamond
    }
}

package com.example.ui.screens

import android.app.Activity

import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.vector.ImageVector
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.credentials.ClearCredentialStateRequest
import androidx.credentials.CredentialManager
import androidx.credentials.CustomCredential
import androidx.credentials.GetCredentialRequest
import androidx.credentials.exceptions.GetCredentialCancellationException

import com.example.ui.theme.NeonCyan
