@OptIn(ExperimentalLayoutApi::class)
@Composable
fun DashboardScreen(
    viewModel: DashboardViewModel,
    onNavigateToPlay: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsState()
    val palette = LocalThemePalette.current

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
            .testTag("dashboard_screen"),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        // Top Header
        item {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                Text(
                    text = "Player Dashboard",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = palette.cellGivenText
                )
                Text(
                    text = "Performance overview and complete history of every puzzle solved",
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = palette.cellNoteText
                )
            }
        }

        // Overview KPI Cards
        item {
            PerformanceSummaryGrid(uiState = uiState, palette = palette)
        }

        // Difficulty Breakdown Section
        item {
            DifficultyBreakdownCard(statsList = uiState.statsList, palette = palette)
        }

        // Every Puzzle Solved History Header & Filters
        item {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.History,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = palette.primary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "Every Solved Puzzle",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = palette.cellGivenText
                        )
                    }

                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                            .background(palette.primary.copy(alpha = 0.15f))
                            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "${uiState.allSolvedPuzzles.size} Solved",
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = palette.primary
                        )
                    }
                }

                // Filter Chips
                FlowRow(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                ) {
                    listOf("ALL", "EASY", "MEDIUM", "HARD", "EXPERT", "DAILY", "FLAWLESS").forEach { filterKey ->
                        val isSelected = (uiState.selectedFilter.equals(filterKey, ignoreCase = true))
                        FilterChip(
                            selected = isSelected,
                            onClick = { viewModel.setFilter(filterKey) },
                            label = {
                                Text(
                                    text = if (filterKey == "ALL") "All (${uiState.allSolvedPuzzles.size})"
                                    else if (filterKey == "FLAWLESS") "Flawless ⭐"
                                    else filterKey.lowercase().replaceFirstChar { it.uppercase() },
                                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                                    fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                                )
                            },
                            colors = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors(
                                selectedContainerColor = palette.primary.copy(alpha = 0.25f),
                                selectedLabelColor = palette.primary,
                                containerColor = palette.card,
                                labelColor = palette.cellNoteText
                            ),
                            border = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipBorder(
                                enabled = true,
                                selected = isSelected,
                                selectedBorderColor = palette.primary,
                                borderColor = palette.border
                            ),
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("filter_chip_${filterKey.lowercase()}")
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Solved Puzzle Items or Empty State
        if (uiState.filteredSolvedPuzzles.isEmpty()) {
            item {
                EmptySolvedPuzzlesCard(
                    palette = palette,
                    onPlayMatch = onNavigateToPlay
                )
            }
        } else {
            items(uiState.filteredSolvedPuzzles, key = { it.id }) { puzzle ->
                SolvedPuzzleItemCard(puzzle = puzzle, palette = palette)
            }
        }

        item {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(20.dp))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun PerformanceSummaryGrid(
    uiState: com.example.ui.viewmodel.DashboardUiState,
    palette: com.example.ui.theme.ThemePalette
) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .testTag("kpi_summary_grid"),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = palette.surface),
        border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, palette.borderStrong)
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
        ) {
            // Row 1: Solved & Score
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                KpiItemBox(
                    title = "Puzzles Solved",
                    value = "${uiState.totalSolved}",
                    icon = Icons.Default.CheckCircle,
                    accentColor = EmeraldGreen,
                    palette = palette,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                )

                KpiItemBox(
                    title = "Total Rating",
                    value = "${uiState.totalScore} pts",
                    icon = Icons.Default.Star,
                    accentColor = WarmAmber,
                    palette = palette,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                )
            }

            // Row 2: Win Rate & Best Time
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                KpiItemBox(
                    title = "Win Rate",
                    value = String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f%%", uiState.winRatePercent),
                    icon = Icons.Default.EmojiEvents,
                    accentColor = palette.primary,
                    palette = palette,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                )

                KpiItemBox(
                    title = "Fastest Solve",
                    value = uiState.bestTimeFormatted,
                    icon = Icons.Default.Bolt,
                    accentColor = NeonCyan,
                    palette = palette,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                )
            }

            // Row 3: Streak & Flawless
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                KpiItemBox(
                    title = "Current Streak",
                    value = "🔥 ${uiState.currentStreak} Days",
                    icon = Icons.Default.LocalFireDepartment,
                    accentColor = palette.secondary,
                    palette = palette,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                )

                KpiItemBox(
                    title = "Flawless Solves",
                    value = "${uiState.flawlessCount}",
                    icon = Icons.Default.CheckCircle,
                    accentColor = EmeraldGreen,
                    palette = palette,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = icon,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = accentColor,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                )
                Text(
                    text = title,
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                    color = palette.cellNoteText
                )
            }
            Text(
                text = value,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                color = palette.cellGivenText
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun DifficultyBreakdownCard(
    statsList: List<GameStatsEntity>,
    palette: com.example.ui.theme.ThemePalette
) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .testTag("difficulty_breakdown_card"),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = palette.surface),
        border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, palette.borderStrong)
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            Text(
                text = "Difficulty Performance Matrix",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = palette.cellGivenText
            )

            val difficulties = listOf("EASY", "MEDIUM", "HARD", "EXPERT", "DAILY")
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                difficulties.forEach { diff ->
                    val stat = statsList.firstOrNull { it.difficulty.equals(diff, ignoreCase = true) }
                    val played = stat?.gamesPlayed ?: 0
                    val won = stat?.gamesWon ?: 0
                    val bestSecs = stat?.bestTimeSeconds ?: 0L
                    val bestFormatted = if (bestSecs > 0) formatTimer(bestSecs) else "--:--"

                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                            .background(palette.card)
                            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(8.dp)
                                    .clip(CircleShape)
                                    .background(
                                        when (diff) {
                                            "EASY" -> EmeraldGreen
                                            "MEDIUM" -> WarmAmber
                                            "HARD" -> palette.primary
                                            "EXPERT" -> CrimsonError
                                            else -> NeonCyan
                                        }
                                    )
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = diff.lowercase().replaceFirstChar { it.uppercase() },
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                fontSize = 13.sp,
                                color = palette.cellGivenText
                            )
                        }

                        Row(
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "$won / $played won",
                                fontSize = 12.sp,
                                color = palette.cellNoteText
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "Best: $bestFormatted",
                                fontSize = 12.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                color = palette.secondary
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun SolvedPuzzleItemCard(
    puzzle: SolvedPuzzleEntity,
    palette: com.example.ui.theme.ThemePalette
) {
    val diffColor = when (puzzle.difficulty.uppercase(Locale.US)) {
        "EASY" -> EmeraldGreen
        "MEDIUM" -> WarmAmber
        "HARD" -> palette.primary
        "EXPERT" -> CrimsonError
        else -> NeonCyan
    }

    val dateFormatted = remember(puzzle.solvedAt) {
        val sdf = SimpleDateFormat("MMM d, yyyy  h:mm a", Locale.US)
        sdf.format(Date(puzzle.solvedAt))
    }

    Card(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .testTag("solved_puzzle_item_${puzzle.id}"),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = palette.surface),
        border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, palette.border)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(14.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                            .background(diffColor.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = puzzle.difficulty.uppercase(Locale.US),
                            fontSize = 11.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = diffColor
                        )
                    }

                    if (puzzle.isDaily) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                                .background(palette.secondary.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                                .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "Daily",
                                fontSize = 10.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = palette.secondary
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (puzzle.mistakes == 0) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                                .background(EmeraldGreen.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                                .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "Flawless ⭐",
                                fontSize = 10.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = EmeraldGreen
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Timer,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = NeonCyan,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = formatTimer(puzzle.timeSpentSeconds),
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            color = palette.cellGivenText
                        )
                    }

                    Text(
                        text = "•",
                        color = palette.cellNoteText,
                        fontSize = 12.sp
                    )

                    Text(
                        text = "${puzzle.mistakes} mistakes",
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        color = if (puzzle.mistakes == 0) EmeraldGreen else palette.cellNoteText
                    )
                }

                Text(
                    text = dateFormatted,
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                    color = palette.cellNoteText
                )
            }

            // Score Earned Badge
            Column(
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "+${puzzle.scoreEarned} pts",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                    color = WarmAmber
                )
                Text(
                    text = "Score Earned",
                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                    color = palette.cellNoteText
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun EmptySolvedPuzzlesCard(
    palette: com.example.ui.theme.ThemePalette,

) {
    Card(

    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(24.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(54.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(palette.primary.copy(alpha = 0.15f)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.History,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = palette.primary,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                )
            }

            Text(
                text = "No Solved Puzzles Recorded Yet",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = palette.cellGivenText
            )

            Text(
                text = "Solve your first Sudoku match to see detailed stats, speed bonus breakdowns, and your complete solving history here!",
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                color = palette.cellNoteText,
                textAlign = androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign.Center
            )

            Button(
                onClick = onPlayMatch,
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = palette.primary),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("empty_dashboard_play_button")
            ) {
                Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.PlayArrow, contentDescription = null)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                Text("Play a Match Now", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.example.data.model

import com.google.firebase.Timestamp
import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentId
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.IgnoreExtraProperties
import com.google.firebase.firestore.PropertyName
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ServerTimestamp

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class FirestoreGameSession(
    @DocumentId val id: String = "active",
    val userId: String = "",
    val difficulty: String = "EASY",
    val timerSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val mistakes: Int = 0,
    @get:PropertyName("isPaused") @set:PropertyName("isPaused")
    var isPaused: Boolean = false,
    @get:PropertyName("isDaily") @set:PropertyName("isDaily")
    var isDaily: Boolean = false,
    val boardData: String = "0:0:0",
    val solutionData: String = "1",
    val notesData: String = "",
    @ServerTimestamp val createdAt: Timestamp? = null,
    @ServerTimestamp val updatedAt: Timestamp? = null
) {
    fun toEntity(): GameSessionEntity {
        return GameSessionEntity(
            id = 1,
            difficulty = difficulty,
            timerSeconds = timerSeconds,
            mistakes = mistakes,
            isPaused = isPaused,
            isDaily = isDaily,
            boardData = boardData,
            solutionData = solutionData,
            notesData = notesData,
            lastUpdated = (updatedAt ?: createdAt)?.toDate()?.time ?: System.currentTimeMillis()
        )
    }

    fun toCreateMap(uid: String): Map<String, Any> {
        return mapOf(
            "userId" to uid,
            "difficulty" to difficulty,
            "timerSeconds" to timerSeconds,
            "mistakes" to mistakes,
            "isPaused" to isPaused,
            "isDaily" to isDaily,
            "boardData" to boardData,
            "solutionData" to solutionData,
            "notesData" to notesData,
            "createdAt" to (createdAt ?: FieldValue.serverTimestamp()),
            "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        ).filterValues { it != null }
    }
}

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class FirestoreDailyChallenge(
    @DocumentId val id: String = "",
    val userId: String = "",
    val dateString: String = "",
    val difficulty: String = "DAILY",
    val completed: Boolean = false,
    val timeSpentSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val mistakes: Int = 0,
    val starsAwarded: Int = 0,
    @ServerTimestamp val createdAt: Timestamp? = null,
    @ServerTimestamp val updatedAt: Timestamp? = null
) {
    fun toEntity(): DailyChallengeEntity {
        return DailyChallengeEntity(
            dateString = dateString.ifBlank { id },
            difficulty = difficulty,
            completed = completed,
            timeSpentSeconds = timeSpentSeconds,
            mistakes = mistakes,
            starsAwarded = starsAwarded,
            completedAt = (updatedAt ?: createdAt)?.toDate()?.time
        )
    }
}

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class FirestoreSticker(
    @DocumentId val id: String = "",
    val userId: String = "",
    val title: String = "",
    val description: String = "",
    val iconName: String = "",
    val category: String = "",
    val unlocked: Boolean = false,
    @ServerTimestamp val createdAt: Timestamp? = null,
    @ServerTimestamp val updatedAt: Timestamp? = null
) {
    fun toEntity(): StickerEntity {
        return StickerEntity(
            id = id,
            title = title,
            description = description,
            iconName = iconName,
            category = category,
            unlocked = unlocked,
            unlockedAt = if (unlocked) (updatedAt ?: createdAt)?.toDate()?.time else null
        )
    }
}

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class FirestoreGameStats(
    @DocumentId val id: String = "",
    val userId: String = "",
    val difficulty: String = "EASY",
    val gamesPlayed: Int = 0,
    val gamesWon: Int = 0,
    val bestTimeSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val totalTimeSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val winStreak: Int = 0,
    val perfectGames: Int = 0,
    @ServerTimestamp val createdAt: Timestamp? = null,
    @ServerTimestamp val updatedAt: Timestamp? = null
) {
    fun toEntity(): GameStatsEntity {
        return GameStatsEntity(
            difficulty = difficulty.ifBlank { id },
            gamesPlayed = gamesPlayed,
            gamesWon = gamesWon,
            bestTimeSeconds = bestTimeSeconds,
            totalTimeSeconds = totalTimeSeconds,
            winStreak = winStreak,
            perfectGames = perfectGames
        )
    }
}

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class FirestoreLeaderboardEntry(
    @DocumentId val id: String = "",
    val userId: String = "",
    val username: String = "",
    val score: Int = 0,
    val gamesWon: Int = 0,
    val winStreak: Int = 0,
    val bestTimeSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val bestTime: String = "--:--",
    val title: String = "Obsidian Solver",
    @ServerTimestamp val updatedAt: Timestamp? = null
)

package com.example.data.model

enum class Difficulty(
    val displayName: String,
    val clueCount: Int,
    val stars: Int,
    val description: String = "",
    val maxMistakes: Int = 3
) {
    EASY("Easy", 40, 1, "Ideal for beginners with plenty of starting clues.", 5),
    MEDIUM("Medium", 32, 2, "Balanced challenge requiring standard logic deductions.", 3),
    HARD("Hard", 28, 3, "Demanding puzzle requiring hidden singles & pairs.", 3),
    EXPERT("Expert", 24, 4, "Master-level puzzle requiring advanced logic techniques.", 3),
    DAILY("Daily Challenge", 30, 3, "Daily competitive puzzle with streaks & rankings.", 3)
}

data class MoveValidationResult(
    val isValidRule: Boolean,
    val isCorrectSolution: Boolean,
    val conflictingCells: List<Pair<Int, Int>> = emptyList(),
    val conflictReason: String? = null
)

data class Cell(
    val row: Int,
    val col: Int,
    val value: Int = 0,
    val isGiven: Boolean = false,
    val notes: Set<Int> = emptySet(),
    val isError: Boolean = false,
    val isHinted: Boolean = false
)

data class GameMove(
    val row: Int,
    val col: Int,
    val oldValue: Int,
    val newValue: Int,
    val oldNotes: Set<Int>,
    val newNotes: Set<Int>
)

data class HintResult(
    val row: Int,
    val col: Int,
    val targetValue: Int,
    val explanation: String
)

    @PrimaryKey val id: Int = 1,
    val difficulty: String,
    val timerSeconds: Long,
    val mistakes: Int,
    val isPaused: Boolean,
    val isDaily: Boolean,
    val boardData: String,
    val solutionData: String,
    val notesData: String,

)

data class DailyChallengeEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val dateString: String,
    val difficulty: String,
    val completed: Boolean,
    val timeSpentSeconds: Long,
    val mistakes: Int,
    val starsAwarded: Int,
    val completedAt: Long? = null
)

data class StickerEntity(

    val title: String,
    val description: String,
    val iconName: String,
    val category: String,
    val unlocked: Boolean,
    val unlockedAt: Long? = null
)

    val totalTimeSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val winStreak: Int = 0,
    val perfectGames: Int = 0
)

    val timeSpentSeconds: Long,
    val mistakes: Int,
    val scoreEarned: Int,
    val isDaily: Boolean,
    val solvedAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/database/dao/Daos.kt
########################################################################################
package com.example.data.database.dao

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import androidx.room.Update
import com.example.data.database.entity.DailyLeaderboardEntity
import com.example.data.database.entity.FriendEntity
import com.example.data.database.entity.GameRecordEntity
import com.example.data.database.entity.StickerEntity
import com.example.data.database.entity.StreakRecordEntity
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Dao
interface GameRecordDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM active_games WHERE id = 1 LIMIT 1")
    fun getActiveGame(): Flow<GameRecordEntity?>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM active_games WHERE id = 1 LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getActiveGameSync(): GameRecordEntity?

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertOrUpdateGame(game: GameRecordEntity)

    @Query("DELETE FROM active_games WHERE id = 1")
    suspend fun clearActiveGame()
}

@Dao
interface StreakDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM streak_record WHERE id = 1 LIMIT 1")
    fun getStreakRecord(): Flow<StreakRecordEntity?>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM streak_record WHERE id = 1 LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getStreakRecordSync(): StreakRecordEntity?

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertOrUpdateStreak(record: StreakRecordEntity)
}

@Dao
interface StickerDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM stickers ORDER BY rarity DESC, id ASC")
    fun getAllStickers(): Flow<List<StickerEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
    suspend fun insertStickers(stickers: List<StickerEntity>)

    @Query("UPDATE stickers SET isUnlocked = 1, unlockedAt = :timestamp WHERE id = :stickerId")
    suspend fun unlockSticker(stickerId: String, timestamp: Long)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM stickers WHERE isUnlocked = 1")
    suspend fun getUnlockedStickersSync(): List<StickerEntity>
}

@Dao
interface FriendDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM friends ORDER BY currentStreak DESC, name ASC")
    fun getAllFriends(): Flow<List<FriendEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertFriends(friends: List<FriendEntity>)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertFriend(friend: FriendEntity)

    @Update
    suspend fun updateFriend(friend: FriendEntity)
}

@Dao
interface LeaderboardDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM daily_leaderboard WHERE dateIso = :date ORDER BY elapsedSeconds ASC, mistakes ASC")
    fun getLeaderboardForDate(date: String): Flow<List<DailyLeaderboardEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertLeaderboardEntries(entries: List<DailyLeaderboardEntity>)
}


########################################################################################
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/database/AppDatabase.kt
########################################################################################
package com.example.data.database

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import com.example.data.database.dao.FriendDao
import com.example.data.database.dao.GameRecordDao
import com.example.data.database.dao.LeaderboardDao
import com.example.data.database.dao.StickerDao
import com.example.data.database.dao.StreakDao
import com.example.data.database.entity.DailyLeaderboardEntity
import com.example.data.database.entity.FriendEntity
import com.example.data.database.entity.GameRecordEntity
import com.example.data.database.entity.StickerEntity
import com.example.data.database.entity.StreakRecordEntity

@Database(
    entities = [
        GameRecordEntity::class,
        StreakRecordEntity::class,
        StickerEntity::class,
        FriendEntity::class,
        DailyLeaderboardEntity::class
    ],
    version = 1,
    exportSchema = false
)
abstract class AppDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun gameDao(): GameRecordDao
    abstract fun streakDao(): StreakDao
    abstract fun stickerDao(): StickerDao
    abstract fun friendDao(): FriendDao
    abstract fun leaderboardDao(): LeaderboardDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: AppDatabase? = null

        fun getDatabase(context: Context): AppDatabase {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                val instance = Room.databaseBuilder(
                    context.applicationContext,
                    AppDatabase::class.java,
                    "obsidian_sudoku.db"
                ).fallbackToDestructiveMigration().build()
                INSTANCE = instance
                instance
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.example.data.database.entity

import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey

@Entity(tableName = "active_games")
data class GameRecordEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: Int = 1,
    val difficulty: String,
    val isDaily: Boolean,
    val dailyDate: String,
    val initialBoard: String,
    val currentBoard: String,
    val solutionBoard: String,
    val notesBoard: String,
    val elapsedSeconds: Long,
    val isPaused: Boolean,
    val mistakes: Int,
    val hintsUsed: Int,
    val isCompleted: Boolean,
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

@Entity(tableName = "streak_record")
data class StreakRecordEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: Int = 1,
    val currentStreak: Int = 0,
    val maxStreak: Int = 0,
    val lastCompletedDate: String = "",
    val completedDailyDates: String = "",
    val totalGamesWon: Int = 0,
    val totalTimePlayedSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val featuredStickerId: String = "novice_spark"
)

@Entity(tableName = "stickers")
data class StickerEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: String,
    val name: String,
    val description: String,
    val iconName: String,
    val rarity: String, // COMMON, RARE, EPIC, LEGENDARY
    val unlockCriteria: String,
    val isUnlocked: Boolean = false,
    val unlockedAt: Long? = null
)

@Entity(tableName = "friends")
data class FriendEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: String,
    val name: String,
    val avatarColor: Long,
    val currentStreak: Int,
    val friendCode: String,
    val lastActiveIso: String,
    val kudosCount: Int = 0
)

@Entity(tableName = "daily_leaderboard")
data class DailyLeaderboardEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: String,
    val dateIso: String,
    val playerName: String,
    val elapsedSeconds: Long,
    val mistakes: Int,
    val rank: Int,
    val isUser: Boolean = false
)

package com.example.data.repository

import android.content.Context
import com.example.R
import com.example.data.local.SudokuDao
import com.example.data.model.DailyChallengeEntity
import com.example.data.model.FirestoreDailyChallenge
import com.example.data.model.FirestoreGameSession
import com.example.data.model.FirestoreGameStats
import com.example.data.model.FirestoreLeaderboardEntry
import com.example.data.model.FirestoreSticker
import com.example.data.model.GameSessionEntity
import com.example.data.model.GameStatsEntity
import com.example.data.model.SolvedPuzzleEntity
import com.example.data.model.StickerEntity
import com.example.data.remote.OperationType
import com.example.data.remote.handleFirestoreError
import com.example.logic.Difficulty
import com.example.logic.ScoreResult
import com.example.logic.SudokuScoring
import com.example.ui.viewmodel.LeaderboardEntry
import com.google.firebase.Firebase
import com.google.firebase.auth.auth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentSnapshot
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.Query
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.awaitClose
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.callbackFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.firstOrNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Calendar
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

class SudokuRepository(
    private val db: FirebaseFirestore,
    private val dao: SudokuDao? = null,
    private val context: Context? = null
) {
    constructor(context: Context, dao: SudokuDao? = null) : this(
        FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(
            context.applicationContext.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
        ),
        dao,
        context.applicationContext
    )

    private val auth
        get() = Firebase.auth

    private fun isLocalOnlySession(targetUserId: String? = null): Boolean {
        return targetUserId == null && auth.currentUser == null && dao != null
    }

    private fun requireUserId(): String {
        return auth.currentUser?.uid
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("User must be signed in with Google before accessing Firestore.")
    }

    private fun currentOrTargetUserId(targetUserId: String?): String {
        return targetUserId ?: auth.currentUser?.uid ?: "unauthenticated"
    }

    fun getPublicUsername(): String {
        val prefs = context?.getSharedPreferences("obsidian_sudoku_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val saved = prefs?.getString("key_public_username", null)
        if (!saved.isNullOrBlank()) return saved

        val currentGoogleName = auth.currentUser?.displayName
        if (!currentGoogleName.isNullOrBlank()) {
            val sanitized = currentGoogleName.replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9_]"), "").take(18)
            if (sanitized.isNotBlank()) return sanitized
        }
        val uidPart = auth.currentUser?.uid?.take(4) ?: "User"
        return "Solver_$uidPart"
    }

    suspend fun setPublicUsername(newUsername: String) {
        val trimmed = newUsername.trim().take(24)
        if (trimmed.isBlank()) return
        context?.getSharedPreferences("obsidian_sudoku_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            ?.edit()
            ?.putString("key_public_username", trimmed)
            ?.apply()

        syncLeaderboardEntry(overrideUsername = trimmed)
    }

    fun getUserTotalScore(): Int {
        val prefs = context?.getSharedPreferences("obsidian_sudoku_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        return prefs?.getInt("key_user_total_score", 0) ?: 0
    }

    private val _userTotalScoreFlow = MutableStateFlow(getUserTotalScore())
    val userTotalScoreFlow: StateFlow<Int> = _userTotalScoreFlow.asStateFlow()

    fun addScore(points: Int): Int {
        val prefs = context?.getSharedPreferences("obsidian_sudoku_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val current = prefs?.getInt("key_user_total_score", 0) ?: 0
        val updated = (current + points).coerceAtLeast(0)
        prefs?.edit()?.putInt("key_user_total_score", updated)?.apply()
        _userTotalScoreFlow.value = updated
        return updated
    }

    fun getOverallStreak(): Int {
        val prefs = context?.getSharedPreferences("obsidian_sudoku_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val today = getTodayDateString()
        val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
        cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -1)
        val yesterday = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.US).format(cal.time)

        val lastWonDate = prefs?.getString("key_last_won_date", "") ?: ""
        val currentStreak = prefs?.getInt("key_overall_streak", 0) ?: 0

        // If last won date is earlier than yesterday, streak is broken
        return when {
            lastWonDate == today || lastWonDate == yesterday -> currentStreak.coerceAtLeast(1)
            lastWonDate.isNotBlank() -> 0
            else -> currentStreak
        }
    }

    private fun updateOverallStreakOnWin(): Int {
        val prefs = context?.getSharedPreferences("obsidian_sudoku_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val today = getTodayDateString()
        val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
        cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -1)
        val yesterday = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.US).format(cal.time)

        val lastWonDate = prefs?.getString("key_last_won_date", "") ?: ""
        val currentStreak = prefs?.getInt("key_overall_streak", 0) ?: 0

        val newStreak = when {
            lastWonDate == yesterday -> currentStreak + 1
            lastWonDate == today -> if (currentStreak > 0) currentStreak else 1
            else -> 1 // Started today
        }

        prefs?.edit()
            ?.putInt("key_overall_streak", newStreak)
            ?.putString("key_last_won_date", today)
            ?.apply()

        return newStreak
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // REALTIME LEADERBOARD
    // -------------------------------------------------------------

    fun observeLeaderboard(): Flow<List<LeaderboardEntry>> = callbackFlow {
        val colRef = db.collection("leaderboard")
        val currentUid = auth.currentUser?.uid

        fun fallbackList(): List<LeaderboardEntry> {
            val myName = getPublicUsername()
            val myStreak = getOverallStreak()
            val currentTotal = getUserTotalScore()
            val userScore = if (currentTotal > 0) currentTotal else (myStreak * 150 + 450).coerceAtLeast(300)
            return listOf(
                LeaderboardEntry(1, "Aetherius", "Grandmaster", 3850, "02:14"),
                LeaderboardEntry(2, "Vespera", "Void Sovereign", 3120, "02:45"),
                LeaderboardEntry(3, myName, "Obsidian Solver", userScore, if (myStreak > 0) "02:50" else "--:--", isCurrentUser = true),
                LeaderboardEntry(4, "Kaelen", "Cipher Adept", 2250, "03:12"),
                LeaderboardEntry(5, "Nyx_99", "Grid Sentinel", 1840, "03:55"),
                LeaderboardEntry(6, "Solaris", "Pencil Artisan", 1430, "04:10"),
                LeaderboardEntry(7, "Zephyr", "Rift Seeker", 980, "05:02")
            ).sortedByDescending { it.score }.mapIndexed { idx, item -> item.copy(rank = idx + 1) }
        }

        if (auth.currentUser == null) {
            trySend(fallbackList())
            awaitClose { }
            return@callbackFlow
        }

        val registration = colRef
            .orderBy("score", Query.Direction.DESCENDING)
            .limit(50)
            .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
                if (error != null) {
                    trySend(fallbackList())
                    return@addSnapshotListener
                }
                if (snapshot == null || snapshot.isEmpty) {
                    trySend(fallbackList())
                    return@addSnapshotListener
                }

                var hasCurrentUserInList = false
                val remoteList = snapshot.documents.mapIndexed { index, doc ->
                    val entryUid = doc.getString("userId") ?: doc.id
                    val username = doc.getString("username") ?: "Solver_${entryUid.take(4)}"
                    val score = (doc.getLong("score") ?: 0L).toInt()
                    val bestTime = doc.getString("bestTime") ?: "--:--"
                    val title = doc.getString("title") ?: "Obsidian Solver"
                    val isCurrent = (currentUid != null && (entryUid == currentUid || doc.id == currentUid))
                    if (isCurrent) hasCurrentUserInList = true

                    LeaderboardEntry(
                        rank = index + 1,
                        name = username,
                        title = title,
                        score = score,
                        bestTime = bestTime,
                        isCurrentUser = isCurrent
                    )
                }.toMutableList()

                // If current user is not in top 50, append local user entry
                if (!hasCurrentUserInList && currentUid != null) {
                    val myName = getPublicUsername()
                    val myStreak = getOverallStreak()
                    val totalScore = getUserTotalScore()
                    val userScore = if (totalScore > 0) totalScore else (myStreak * 150 + 100)
                    remoteList.add(
                        LeaderboardEntry(
                            rank = remoteList.size + 1,
                            name = myName,
                            title = "Obsidian Solver",
                            score = userScore,
                            bestTime = "--:--",
                            isCurrentUser = true
                        )
                    )
                }

                trySend(remoteList)
            }
        awaitClose { registration.remove() }
    }

    suspend fun syncLeaderboardEntry(overrideUsername: String? = null) {
        val uid = auth.currentUser?.uid ?: return
        val username = overrideUsername ?: getPublicUsername()
        val docRef = db.collection("leaderboard").document(uid)

        val allStatsList = try {
            val snap = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("game_stats").get().await()
            snap.documents.mapNotNull { it.toObject(FirestoreGameStats::class.java)?.toEntity() }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            emptyList()
        }

        val totalWon = allStatsList.sumOf { it.gamesWon }
        val totalPerfect = allStatsList.sumOf { it.perfectGames }
        val streak = getOverallStreak()
        val bestTimeSecs = allStatsList.filter { it.bestTimeSeconds > 0 }.minOfOrNull { it.bestTimeSeconds } ?: 0L
        val bestTimeStr = if (bestTimeSecs > 0) {
            val mins = bestTimeSecs / 60
            val secs = bestTimeSecs % 60
            String.format(Locale.US, "%02d:%02d", mins, secs)
        } else "--:--"

        val currentStoredScore = getUserTotalScore()
        val calculatedStatsScore = (totalWon * 200 + totalPerfect * 250 + streak * 75)
        val score = maxOf(currentStoredScore, calculatedStatsScore).coerceAtLeast(100)
        val title = when {
            score >= 5000 -> "Grandmaster"
            score >= 3000 -> "Void Sovereign"
            score >= 1500 -> "Grid Sentinel"
            score >= 500 -> "Cipher Adept"
            else -> "Obsidian Initiate"
        }

        try {
            val existing = docRef.get().await()
            if (existing.exists()) {
                docRef.update(
                    mapOf(
                        "username" to username,
                        "score" to score,
                        "gamesWon" to totalWon,
                        "winStreak" to streak,
                        "bestTimeSeconds" to bestTimeSecs,
                        "bestTime" to bestTimeStr,
                        "title" to title,
                        "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                    )
                ).await()
            } else {
                docRef.set(
                    mapOf(
                        "userId" to uid,
                        "username" to username,
                        "score" to score,
                        "gamesWon" to totalWon,
                        "winStreak" to streak,
                        "bestTimeSeconds" to bestTimeSecs,
                        "bestTime" to bestTimeStr,
                        "title" to title,
                        "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                    )
                ).await()
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            // Firestore write ignored if transient
        }
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // GAME SESSIONS
    // -------------------------------------------------------------

    fun observeActiveSession(targetUserId: String? = null): Flow<GameSessionEntity?> {
        if (isLocalOnlySession(targetUserId)) {
            return dao!!.getActiveSession()
        }
        return callbackFlow {
            val uid = currentOrTargetUserId(targetUserId)
            val docRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("sessions").document("active")
            val registration = docRef.addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
                if (error != null) {
                    handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, docRef.path)
                    close(error)
                    return@addSnapshotListener
                }
                val entity = if (snapshot != null && snapshot.exists()) {
                    snapshot.toObject(
                        FirestoreGameSession::class.java,
                        DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
                    )?.toEntity()
                } else {
                    null
                }
                trySend(entity)
            }
            awaitClose { registration.remove() }
        }
    }

    fun observeDailyChallenges(targetUserId: String? = null): Flow<List<DailyChallengeEntity>> {
        if (isLocalOnlySession(targetUserId)) {
            return dao!!.getAllDailyChallenges()
        }
        return callbackFlow {
            val uid = currentOrTargetUserId(targetUserId)
            val colRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("daily_challenges")
            val registration = colRef.whereEqualTo("userId", uid)
                .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
                    if (error != null) {
                        handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.LIST, colRef.path)
                        close(error)
                        return@addSnapshotListener
                    }
                    val list = snapshot?.documents?.mapNotNull { doc ->
                        doc.toObject(
                            FirestoreDailyChallenge::class.java,
                            DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
                        )?.toEntity()
                    }?.sortedByDescending { it.dateString } ?: emptyList()
                    trySend(list)
                }
            awaitClose { registration.remove() }
        }
    }

    fun observeStickers(targetUserId: String? = null): Flow<List<StickerEntity>> {
        if (isLocalOnlySession(targetUserId)) {
            return dao!!.getAllStickers()
        }
        return callbackFlow {
            val uid = currentOrTargetUserId(targetUserId)
            val colRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("stickers")
            val registration = colRef.whereEqualTo("userId", uid)
                .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
                    if (error != null) {
                        handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.LIST, colRef.path)
                        close(error)
                        return@addSnapshotListener
                    }
                    val list = snapshot?.documents?.mapNotNull { doc ->
                        doc.toObject(
                            FirestoreSticker::class.java,
                            DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
                        )?.toEntity()
                    } ?: emptyList()
                    trySend(list)
                }
            awaitClose { registration.remove() }
        }
    }

    fun observeAllStats(targetUserId: String? = null): Flow<List<GameStatsEntity>> {
        if (isLocalOnlySession(targetUserId)) {
            return dao!!.getAllStats()
        }
        return callbackFlow {
            val uid = currentOrTargetUserId(targetUserId)
            val colRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("game_stats")
            val registration = colRef.whereEqualTo("userId", uid)
                .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
                    if (error != null) {
                        handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.LIST, colRef.path)
                        close(error)
                        return@addSnapshotListener
                    }
                    val list = snapshot?.documents?.mapNotNull { doc ->
                        doc.toObject(
                            FirestoreGameStats::class.java,
                            DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
                        )?.toEntity()
                    } ?: emptyList()
                    trySend(list)
                }
            awaitClose { registration.remove() }
        }
    }

    val activeSession: Flow<GameSessionEntity?>
        get() = observeActiveSession()

    val allDailyChallenges: Flow<List<DailyChallengeEntity>>
        get() = observeDailyChallenges()

    val stickers: Flow<List<StickerEntity>>
        get() = observeStickers()

    val unlockedStickersCount: Flow<Int>
        get() = observeStickers().map { list -> list.count { it.unlocked } }

    val allStats: Flow<List<GameStatsEntity>>
        get() = observeAllStats()

    val allSolvedPuzzles: Flow<List<SolvedPuzzleEntity>>
        get() = dao?.getAllSolvedPuzzles() ?: kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf(emptyList())

    val solvedPuzzlesCount: Flow<Int>
        get() = dao?.getSolvedPuzzlesCount() ?: kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf(0)

    suspend fun getActiveSessionOnce(targetUserId: String? = null): GameSessionEntity? {
        if (isLocalOnlySession(targetUserId)) {
            return dao!!.getActiveSession().firstOrNull()
        }
        val uid = currentOrTargetUserId(targetUserId)
        val docRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("sessions").document("active")
        return try {
            val snap = docRef.get().await()
            if (snap.exists()) {
                snap.toObject(
                    FirestoreGameSession::class.java,
                    DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
                )?.toEntity()
            } else {
                null
            }
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, docRef.path)
            throw e
        }
    }

    suspend fun initDefaultStickers() {
        val defaultList = defaultStickerCatalog()
        if (isLocalOnlySession()) {
            dao?.insertStickers(defaultList)
            return
        }
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val colRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("stickers")
        try {
            val existingSnap = colRef.whereEqualTo("userId", uid).get().await()
            val existingIds = existingSnap.documents.map { it.id }.toSet()
            for (sticker in defaultList) {
                if (!existingIds.contains(sticker.id)) {
                    val docRef = colRef.document(sticker.id)
                    val payload = mapOf(
                        "userId" to uid,
                        "title" to sticker.title,
                        "description" to sticker.description,
                        "iconName" to sticker.iconName,
                        "category" to sticker.category,
                        "unlocked" to sticker.unlocked,
                        "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                        "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                    )
                    docRef.set(payload).await()
                }
            }
            dao?.insertStickers(defaultList)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, colRef.path)
            throw e
        }
    }

    suspend fun saveActiveSession(
        difficulty: String,
        timerSeconds: Long,
        mistakes: Int,
        isPaused: Boolean,
        isDaily: Boolean,
        boardData: String,
        solutionData: String,
        notesData: String
    ) {
        val safeBoard = boardData.ifBlank { "0:0:0" }
        val safeSolution = solutionData.ifBlank { "1" }
        if (isLocalOnlySession()) {
            dao?.saveActiveSession(
                GameSessionEntity(
                    id = 1,
                    difficulty = difficulty,
                    timerSeconds = timerSeconds,
                    mistakes = mistakes,
                    isPaused = isPaused,
                    isDaily = isDaily,
                    boardData = safeBoard,
                    solutionData = safeSolution,
                    notesData = notesData,
                    lastUpdated = System.currentTimeMillis()
                )
            )
            return
        }
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val docRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("sessions").document("active")
        try {
            val existing = docRef.get().await()
            if (existing.exists()) {
                val updatePayload = mapOf(
                    "difficulty" to difficulty,
                    "timerSeconds" to timerSeconds,
                    "mistakes" to mistakes,
                    "isPaused" to isPaused,
                    "isDaily" to isDaily,
                    "boardData" to safeBoard,
                    "solutionData" to safeSolution,
                    "notesData" to notesData,
                    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                )
                docRef.update(updatePayload).await()
            } else {
                val createPayload = mapOf(
                    "userId" to uid,
                    "difficulty" to difficulty,
                    "timerSeconds" to timerSeconds,
                    "mistakes" to mistakes,
                    "isPaused" to isPaused,
                    "isDaily" to isDaily,
                    "boardData" to safeBoard,
                    "solutionData" to safeSolution,
                    "notesData" to notesData,
                    "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                )
                docRef.set(createPayload).await()
            }
            dao?.saveActiveSession(
                GameSessionEntity(
                    id = 1,
                    difficulty = difficulty,
                    timerSeconds = timerSeconds,
                    mistakes = mistakes,
                    isPaused = isPaused,
                    isDaily = isDaily,
                    boardData = safeBoard,
                    solutionData = safeSolution,
                    notesData = notesData,
                    lastUpdated = System.currentTimeMillis()
                )
            )
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, docRef.path)
            throw e
        }
    }

    suspend fun clearActiveSession() {
        if (isLocalOnlySession()) {
            dao?.clearActiveSession()
            return
        }
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val docRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("sessions").document("active")
        try {
            docRef.delete().await()
            dao?.clearActiveSession()
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.DELETE, docRef.path)
            throw e
        }
    }

    /**
     * Records a finished Sudoku game.
     * Calculates transparent match score, persists solved puzzle entry, updates streak,
     * and syncs the realtime leaderboard.
     */
    suspend fun recordGameFinished(
        difficulty: String,
        timeSeconds: Long,
        mistakes: Int,
        isWon: Boolean
    ): ScoreResult {
        val diffEnum = Difficulty.fromName(difficulty)
        val newStreak = if (isWon) {
            updateOverallStreakOnWin()
        } else {
            0
        }

        val scoreResult = SudokuScoring.calculateScore(
            difficulty = diffEnum,
            timeSeconds = timeSeconds,
            mistakes = mistakes,
            streak = newStreak,
            isWon = isWon
        )

        // Increment cumulative score immediately!
        addScore(scoreResult.totalEarned)

        // Save individual solved puzzle history entry
        if (isWon) {
            val solvedPuzzle = SolvedPuzzleEntity(
                difficulty = difficulty,
                timeSpentSeconds = timeSeconds,
                mistakes = mistakes,
                scoreEarned = scoreResult.totalEarned,
                isDaily = (diffEnum == Difficulty.DAILY),
                solvedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
            )
            try {
                dao?.insertSolvedPuzzle(solvedPuzzle)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }

        if (isLocalOnlySession()) {
            val localDao = dao ?: return scoreResult
            val existing = localDao.getStats(difficulty) ?: GameStatsEntity(difficulty = difficulty)
            val newPlayed = existing.gamesPlayed + 1
            val newWon = if (isWon) existing.gamesWon + 1 else existing.gamesWon
            val newPerfect = if (isWon && mistakes == 0) existing.perfectGames + 1 else existing.perfectGames
            val newBestTime = if (isWon) {
                if (existing.bestTimeSeconds == 0L || timeSeconds < existing.bestTimeSeconds) timeSeconds else existing.bestTimeSeconds
            } else existing.bestTimeSeconds
            val newTotalTime = existing.totalTimeSeconds + timeSeconds

            localDao.saveStats(
                existing.copy(
                    gamesPlayed = newPlayed,
                    gamesWon = newWon,
                    bestTimeSeconds = newBestTime,
                    totalTimeSeconds = newTotalTime,
                    winStreak = newStreak,
                    perfectGames = newPerfect
                )
            )
            if (isWon) {
                localDao.unlockSticker("first_win")
                if (timeSeconds < 300) localDao.unlockSticker("speed_demon")
                if (mistakes == 0) localDao.unlockSticker("flawless")
                if (difficulty == "EXPERT") localDao.unlockSticker("expert_solver")
                if (newStreak >= 3) localDao.unlockSticker("daily_streak_3")
                if (newStreak >= 7) localDao.unlockSticker("daily_streak_7")
                if (newWon >= 10) localDao.unlockSticker("ten_games")
            }
            return scoreResult
        }

        val uid = requireUserId()
        val docRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("game_stats").document(difficulty)
        try {
            val snap = docRef.get().await()
            val existing = if (snap.exists()) {
                snap.toObject(
                    FirestoreGameStats::class.java,
                    DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
                )?.toEntity() ?: GameStatsEntity(difficulty = difficulty)
            } else {
                GameStatsEntity(difficulty = difficulty)
            }

            val newPlayed = existing.gamesPlayed + 1
            val newWon = if (isWon) existing.gamesWon + 1 else existing.gamesWon
            val newPerfect = if (isWon && mistakes == 0) existing.perfectGames + 1 else existing.perfectGames
            val newBestTime = if (isWon) {
                if (existing.bestTimeSeconds == 0L || timeSeconds < existing.bestTimeSeconds) {
                    timeSeconds
                } else {
                    existing.bestTimeSeconds
                }
            } else {
                existing.bestTimeSeconds
            }
            val newTotalTime = existing.totalTimeSeconds + timeSeconds

            if (snap.exists()) {
                val updatePayload = mapOf(
                    "gamesPlayed" to newPlayed,
                    "gamesWon" to newWon,
                    "bestTimeSeconds" to newBestTime,
                    "totalTimeSeconds" to newTotalTime,
                    "winStreak" to newStreak,
                    "perfectGames" to newPerfect,
                    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                )
                docRef.update(updatePayload).await()
            } else {
                val createPayload = mapOf(
                    "userId" to uid,
                    "difficulty" to difficulty,
                    "gamesPlayed" to newPlayed,
                    "gamesWon" to newWon,
                    "bestTimeSeconds" to newBestTime,
                    "totalTimeSeconds" to newTotalTime,
                    "winStreak" to newStreak,
                    "perfectGames" to newPerfect,
                    "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                )
                docRef.set(createPayload).await()
            }

            dao?.saveStats(
                existing.copy(
                    gamesPlayed = newPlayed,
                    gamesWon = newWon,
                    bestTimeSeconds = newBestTime,
                    totalTimeSeconds = newTotalTime,
                    winStreak = newStreak,
                    perfectGames = newPerfect
                )
            )

            if (isWon) {
                unlockSticker("first_win")
                if (timeSeconds < 300) unlockSticker("speed_demon")
                if (mistakes == 0) unlockSticker("flawless")
                if (difficulty == "EXPERT") unlockSticker("expert_solver")
                if (newStreak >= 3) unlockSticker("daily_streak_3")
                if (newStreak >= 7) unlockSticker("daily_streak_7")
                if (newWon >= 10) unlockSticker("ten_games")
            }

            // Sync score and stats with realtime leaderboard on every match!
            syncLeaderboardEntry()
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, docRef.path)
            throw e
        }
        return scoreResult
    }

    suspend fun completeDailyChallenge(
        dateString: String,
        difficulty: String,
        timeSeconds: Long,
        mistakes: Int,
        stars: Int
    ) {
        if (isLocalOnlySession()) {
            dao?.saveDailyChallenge(
                DailyChallengeEntity(
                    dateString = dateString,
                    difficulty = difficulty,
                    completed = true,
                    timeSpentSeconds = timeSeconds,
                    mistakes = mistakes,
                    starsAwarded = stars,
                    completedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
                )
            )
            return
        }
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val docRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("daily_challenges").document(dateString)
        try {
            val snap = docRef.get().await()
            if (snap.exists()) {
                val updatePayload = mapOf(
                    "difficulty" to difficulty,
                    "completed" to true,
                    "timeSpentSeconds" to timeSeconds,
                    "mistakes" to mistakes,
                    "starsAwarded" to stars,
                    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                )
                docRef.update(updatePayload).await()
            } else {
                val createPayload = mapOf(
                    "userId" to uid,
                    "dateString" to dateString,
                    "difficulty" to difficulty,
                    "completed" to true,
                    "timeSpentSeconds" to timeSeconds,
                    "mistakes" to mistakes,
                    "starsAwarded" to stars,
                    "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                )
                docRef.set(createPayload).await()
            }

            dao?.saveDailyChallenge(
                DailyChallengeEntity(
                    dateString = dateString,
                    difficulty = difficulty,
                    completed = true,
                    timeSpentSeconds = timeSeconds,
                    mistakes = mistakes,
                    starsAwarded = stars,
                    completedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
                )
            )
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, docRef.path)
            throw e
        }
    }

    suspend fun unlockSticker(id: String) {
        if (isLocalOnlySession()) {
            dao?.unlockSticker(id)
            return
        }
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val docRef = db.collection("users").document(uid).collection("stickers").document(id)
        try {
            val snap = docRef.get().await()
            if (snap.exists()) {
                docRef.update(
                    mapOf(
                        "unlocked" to true,
                        "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                    )
                ).await()
            } else {
                val template = defaultStickerCatalog().firstOrNull { it.id == id }
                    ?: StickerEntity(
                        id = id,
                        title = id,
                        description = "Achievement unlocked",
                        iconName = "sparkles",
                        category = "Milestone",
                        unlocked = true
                    )
                docRef.set(
                    mapOf(
                        "userId" to uid,
                        "title" to template.title,
                        "description" to template.description,
                        "iconName" to template.iconName,
                        "category" to template.category,
                        "unlocked" to true,
                        "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                        "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                    )
                ).await()
            }
            dao?.unlockSticker(id)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, docRef.path)
            throw e
        }
    }

    fun getTodayDateString(): String {
        val sdf = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.US)
        return sdf.format(Date())
    }

    fun calculateStreak(challenges: List<DailyChallengeEntity>): Int {
        val overall = getOverallStreak()
        if (overall > 0) return overall

        if (challenges.isEmpty()) return 0
        val completedDates = challenges.filter { it.completed }.map { it.dateString }.toSet()
        val sdf = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.US)
        val cal = Calendar.getInstance()

        var streak = 0
        val todayStr = sdf.format(cal.time)
        cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -1)
        val yesterdayStr = sdf.format(cal.time)

        val checkCal = Calendar.getInstance()
        if (completedDates.contains(todayStr)) {
            streak++
            checkCal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -1)
        } else if (completedDates.contains(yesterdayStr)) {
            checkCal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -1)
        } else {
            return 0
        }

        while (true) {
            val dateStr = sdf.format(checkCal.time)
            if (completedDates.contains(dateStr)) {
                if (dateStr != todayStr) streak++
                checkCal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -1)
            } else {
                break
            }
        }
        return streak
    }

    companion object {
        fun defaultStickerCatalog(): List<StickerEntity> = listOf(
            StickerEntity(
                id = "first_win",
                title = "Obsidian Initiate",
                description = "Complete your first Sudoku puzzle",
                iconName = "sparkles",
                category = "Milestone",
                unlocked = false
            ),
            StickerEntity(
                id = "speed_demon",
                title = "Void Runner",
                description = "Solve any puzzle in under 5 minutes",
                iconName = "bolt",
                category = "Speed",
                unlocked = false
            ),
            StickerEntity(
                id = "flawless",
                title = "Obsidian Crystal",
                description = "Complete a puzzle with zero mistakes",
                iconName = "diamond",
                category = "Precision",
                unlocked = false
            ),
            StickerEntity(
                id = "expert_solver",
                title = "Grandmaster Shard",
                description = "Master an Expert difficulty puzzle",
                iconName = "military_tech",
                category = "Difficulty",
                unlocked = false
            ),
            StickerEntity(
                id = "daily_streak_3",
                title = "Starlight Continuum",
                description = "Reach a 3-day daily challenge streak",
                iconName = "local_fire_department",
                category = "Streaks",
                unlocked = false
            ),
            StickerEntity(
                id = "daily_streak_7",
                title = "Eternal Eclipse",
                description = "Reach a 7-day daily challenge streak",
                iconName = "brightness_auto",
                category = "Streaks",
                unlocked = false
            ),
            StickerEntity(

                iconName = "edit_note",

            StickerEntity(

    }

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/remote/FirestoreErrorHandler.kt
########################################################################################
package com.example.data.remote

import android.util.Log
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

enum class OperationType(val value: String) {
    CREATE("create"),
    UPDATE("update"),
    DELETE("delete"),
    LIST("list"),
    GET("get"),
    WRITE("write"),
}

fun handleFirestoreError(exception: Exception, operationType: OperationType, path: String?): String {
    val auth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance()
    val currentUser = auth.currentUser

    val providerInfoList = currentUser?.providerData?.map { provider ->
        JSONObject().apply {
            put("providerId", provider.providerId)
            put("email", provider.email)
        }
    } ?: emptyList()

    val authInfoJson = JSONObject().apply {
        put("userId", currentUser?.uid)
        put("email", currentUser?.email)
        put("emailVerified", currentUser?.isEmailVerified)
        put("tenantId", currentUser?.tenantId)
        put("providerInfo", JSONArray(providerInfoList))
    }

    val errorInfoJson = JSONObject().apply {
        put("error", exception.message ?: exception.toString())
        put("operationType", operationType.value)
        put("path", path)
        put("authInfo", authInfoJson)
    }

    val jsonString = errorInfoJson.toString()
    Log.e("FirestoreError", "Firestore Error: $jsonString")
    return jsonString
}

package com.example.data.local

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import androidx.room.Update
import com.example.data.model.DailyChallengeEntity
import com.example.data.model.GameSessionEntity
import com.example.data.model.GameStatsEntity
import com.example.data.model.SolvedPuzzleEntity
import com.example.data.model.StickerEntity
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Dao
interface SudokuDao {

    // Active session
    @Query("SELECT * FROM game_sessions WHERE id = 1 LIMIT 1")
    fun getActiveSession(): Flow<GameSessionEntity?>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun saveActiveSession(session: GameSessionEntity)

    @Query("DELETE FROM game_sessions WHERE id = 1")
    suspend fun clearActiveSession()

    // Daily challenges
    @Query("SELECT * FROM daily_challenges ORDER BY dateString DESC")
    fun getAllDailyChallenges(): Flow<List<DailyChallengeEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM daily_challenges WHERE dateString = :date LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getDailyChallenge(date: String): DailyChallengeEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM daily_challenges WHERE dateString = :date LIMIT 1")
    fun observeDailyChallenge(date: String): Flow<DailyChallengeEntity?>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun saveDailyChallenge(challenge: DailyChallengeEntity)

    // Stickers
    @Query("SELECT * FROM stickers ORDER BY unlocked DESC, id ASC")
    fun getAllStickers(): Flow<List<StickerEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stickers WHERE unlocked = 1")
    fun getUnlockedStickersCount(): Flow<Int>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
    suspend fun insertStickers(stickers: List<StickerEntity>)

    @Update
    suspend fun updateSticker(sticker: StickerEntity)

    @Query("UPDATE stickers SET unlocked = 1, unlockedAt = :timestamp WHERE id = :id AND unlocked = 0")
    suspend fun unlockSticker(id: String, timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Int

    // Stats
    @Query("SELECT * FROM game_stats WHERE difficulty = :difficulty LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getStats(difficulty: String): GameStatsEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM game_stats")
    fun getAllStats(): Flow<List<GameStatsEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun saveStats(stats: GameStatsEntity)

    // Solved Puzzles History
    @Query("SELECT * FROM solved_puzzles ORDER BY solvedAt DESC")
    fun getAllSolvedPuzzles(): Flow<List<SolvedPuzzleEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM solved_puzzles WHERE difficulty = :difficulty ORDER BY solvedAt DESC")
    fun getSolvedPuzzlesByDifficulty(difficulty: String): Flow<List<SolvedPuzzleEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM solved_puzzles")
    fun getSolvedPuzzlesCount(): Flow<Int>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSolvedPuzzle(puzzle: SolvedPuzzleEntity): Long
}
