package com.example.data.local

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import com.example.data.model.DailyChallengeEntity
import com.example.data.model.GameSessionEntity
import com.example.data.model.GameStatsEntity
import com.example.data.model.SolvedPuzzleEntity
import com.example.data.model.StickerEntity

@Database(
    entities = [
        GameSessionEntity::class,
        DailyChallengeEntity::class,
        StickerEntity::class,
        GameStatsEntity::class,
        SolvedPuzzleEntity::class
    ],
    version = 2,
    exportSchema = false
)
abstract class SudokuDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun sudokuDao(): SudokuDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: SudokuDatabase? = null

        fun getDatabase(context: Context): SudokuDatabase {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                val instance = Room.databaseBuilder(
                    context.applicationContext,
                    SudokuDatabase::class.java,
                    "obsidian_sudoku.db"
                ).fallbackToDestructiveMigration().build()
                INSTANCE = instance
                instance
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/logic/SudokuEngine.kt
########################################################################################
package com.example.logic

import kotlin.random.Random

enum class Difficulty(
    val displayName: String,
    val clues: Int,
    val maxMistakes: Int,
    val stars: Int,
    val description: String
) {
    EASY(
        displayName = "Easy",
        clues = 40,
        maxMistakes = 5,
        stars = 1,
        description = "Relaxed flow with plenty of clues"
    ),
    MEDIUM(
        displayName = "Medium",
        clues = 32,
        maxMistakes = 3,
        stars = 2,
        description = "Balanced logic test for regular solvers"
    ),
    HARD(
        displayName = "Hard",
        clues = 28,
        maxMistakes = 3,
        stars = 3,
        description = "Deep deductions and candidate filtering"
    ),
    EXPERT(
        displayName = "Expert",
        clues = 24,
        maxMistakes = 3,
        stars = 4,
        description = "Pencil marks required, pure mastery"
    ),
    DAILY(
        displayName = "Daily Challenge",
        clues = 30,
        maxMistakes = 3,
        stars = 3,
        description = "Specially calibrated puzzle of the day"
    );

    companion object {
        fun fromName(name: String): Difficulty {
            return entries.firstOrNull { it.name.equals(name, ignoreCase = true) } ?: MEDIUM
        }
    }
}

data class GeneratedPuzzle(
    val initialBoard: Array<IntArray>,
    val solution: Array<IntArray>,
    val difficulty: Difficulty
) {
    override fun equals(other: Any?): Boolean {
        if (this === other) return true
        if (other !is GeneratedPuzzle) return false
        return difficulty == other.difficulty &&
                initialBoard.contentDeepEquals(other.initialBoard) &&
                solution.contentDeepEquals(other.solution)
    }

    override fun hashCode(): Int {
        var result = initialBoard.contentDeepHashCode()
        result = 31 * result + solution.contentDeepHashCode()
        result = 31 * result + difficulty.hashCode()
        return result
    }
}

data class MoveValidationResult(
    val isValid: Boolean,
    val isCorrectAgainstSolution: Boolean,
    val conflictReason: String? = null,
    val conflictingCoordinates: List<Pair<Int, Int>> = emptyList()
)

data class WinConditionResult(
    val isWon: Boolean,
    val isComplete: Boolean,
    val hasConflicts: Boolean,
    val emptyCellsCount: Int,
    val message: String
)

data class HintResult(
    val row: Int,
    val col: Int,
    val value: Int
)

/**
 * Pure Kotlin Sudoku Game Engine handling:
 * 1. Board generation with guaranteed unique solutions across 5 difficulty levels.
 * 2. Real-time move validation checking row, column, and 3x3 box constraints and solution alignment.
 * 3. Complete win condition verification ensuring all 81 cells are non-zero, compliant with Sudoku rules,
 *    and matching the solution.
 * 4. Helper candidate calculation and hints.
 */
object SudokuEngine {

    const val GRID_SIZE = 9
    const val BOX_SIZE = 3

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. BOARD GENERATION
    // -------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Generates a fully playable Sudoku puzzle with guaranteed unique solution.
     */
    fun generatePuzzle(
        difficulty: Difficulty,
        seed: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
    ): GeneratedPuzzle {
        val random = Random(seed)
        val solution = generateCompletedBoard(random)
        val puzzleBoard = Array(GRID_SIZE) { r -> solution[r].clone() }

        val targetClues = difficulty.clues.coerceIn(17, 60)
        val cellsToRemove = (GRID_SIZE * GRID_SIZE) - targetClues

        val cellCoordinates = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, Int>>()
        for (r in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
                cellCoordinates.add(Pair(r, c))
            }
        }
        cellCoordinates.shuffle(random)

        var removedCount = 0
        for ((r, c) in cellCoordinates) {
            if (removedCount >= cellsToRemove) break

            val temp = puzzleBoard[r][c]
            puzzleBoard[r][c] = 0

            // Check if removing this cell preserves uniqueness
            val copy = Array(GRID_SIZE) { rowIdx -> puzzleBoard[rowIdx].clone() }
            val solutions = countSolutions(copy, limit = 2)
            if (solutions != 1) {
                puzzleBoard[r][c] = temp
            } else {
                removedCount++
            }
        }

        return GeneratedPuzzle(
            initialBoard = puzzleBoard,
            solution = solution,
            difficulty = difficulty
        )
    }

    /**
     * Generates a complete 9x9 Sudoku board with valid digits 1-9 in every row, column, and box.
     */
    fun generateCompletedBoard(random: Random = Random): Array<IntArray> {
        val board = Array(GRID_SIZE) { IntArray(GRID_SIZE) }
        // Fill the 3 independent 3x3 diagonal boxes first
        for (i in 0 until GRID_SIZE step BOX_SIZE) {
            fillDiagonalBox(board, i, i, random)
        }
        // Solve the remaining cells using randomized backtracking
        solveBoard(board, random)
        return board
    }

    private fun fillDiagonalBox(board: Array<IntArray>, startRow: Int, startCol: Int, random: Random) {
        val digits = (1..9).shuffled(random)
        var idx = 0
        for (r in 0 until BOX_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until BOX_SIZE) {
                board[startRow + r][startCol + c] = digits[idx++]
            }
        }
    }

    private fun solveBoard(board: Array<IntArray>, random: Random): Boolean {
        for (r in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
                if (board[r][c] == 0) {
                    val digits = (1..9).shuffled(random)
                    for (num in digits) {
                        if (isSafe(board, r, c, num)) {
                            board[r][c] = num
                            if (solveBoard(board, random)) return true
                            board[r][c] = 0
                        }
                    }
                    return false
                }
            }
        }
        return true
    }

    /**
     * Counts solutions up to [limit] using Minimum Remaining Values (MRV) heuristic.
     */
    fun countSolutions(board: Array<IntArray>, limit: Int = 2): Int {
        var emptyRow = -1
        var emptyCol = -1
        var minPossibilities = 10
        var bestCandidates = listOf<Int>()

        for (r in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
                if (board[r][c] == 0) {
                    val candidates = (1..9).filter { isSafe(board, r, c, it) }
                    if (candidates.size < minPossibilities) {
                        minPossibilities = candidates.size
                        emptyRow = r
                        emptyCol = c
                        bestCandidates = candidates
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (emptyRow == -1) {
            return 1
        }

        var count = 0
        for (num in bestCandidates) {
            board[emptyRow][emptyCol] = num
            count += countSolutions(board, limit - count)
            board[emptyRow][emptyCol] = 0
            if (count >= limit) break
        }
        return count
    }

    /**
     * Checks if placing [num] at [row], [col] does not violate Sudoku constraints.
     */
    fun isSafe(board: Array<IntArray>, row: Int, col: Int, num: Int): Boolean {
        for (c in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            if (board[row][c] == num) return false
        }
        for (r in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            if (board[r][col] == num) return false
        }
        val boxRow = row - row % BOX_SIZE
        val boxCol = col - col % BOX_SIZE
        for (r in 0 until BOX_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until BOX_SIZE) {
                if (board[boxRow + r][boxCol + c] == num) return false
            }
        }
        return true
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. MOVE VALIDATION
    // -------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Validates a player move against current board constraints and unique solution.
     */
    fun validateMove(
        currentBoard: Array<IntArray>,
        solution: Array<IntArray>,
        row: Int,
        col: Int,
        num: Int
    ): MoveValidationResult {
        if (num !in 1..9) {
            return MoveValidationResult(
                isValid = false,
                isCorrectAgainstSolution = false,
                conflictReason = "Digit must be between 1 and 9"
            )
        }

        val conflictingCells = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, Int>>()

        // Row conflict check
        for (c in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            if (c != col && currentBoard[row][c] == num) {
                conflictingCells.add(Pair(row, c))
            }
        }

        // Column conflict check
        for (r in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            if (r != row && currentBoard[r][col] == num) {
                conflictingCells.add(Pair(r, col))
            }
        }

        // 3x3 Box conflict check
        val boxRow = row - row % BOX_SIZE
        val boxCol = col - col % BOX_SIZE
        for (r in 0 until BOX_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until BOX_SIZE) {
                val cr = boxRow + r
                val cc = boxCol + c
                if ((cr != row || cc != col) && currentBoard[cr][cc] == num) {
                    if (!conflictingCells.contains(Pair(cr, cc))) {
                        conflictingCells.add(Pair(cr, cc))
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        val hasBoardConflict = conflictingCells.isNotEmpty()
        val matchesSolution = (solution[row][col] == num)

        val reason = when {
            hasBoardConflict -> "Duplicate $num in row, column, or 3x3 subgrid"
            !matchesSolution -> "Does not match the unique solution"
            else -> null
        }

        return MoveValidationResult(
            isValid = !hasBoardConflict,
            isCorrectAgainstSolution = matchesSolution,
            conflictReason = reason,
            conflictingCoordinates = conflictingCells
        )
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. CHECKING FOR WIN CONDITIONS
    // -------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Comprehensive check for game victory:
     * - Every cell is filled (non-zero).
     * - No duplicate digits in any row, column, or 3x3 box.
     * - Every cell matches the valid solution.
     */
    fun checkWinCondition(
        currentBoard: Array<IntArray>,
        solution: Array<IntArray>
    ): WinConditionResult {
        var emptyCount = 0
        var solutionMismatches = 0

        for (r in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
                val v = currentBoard[r][c]
                if (v == 0) {
                    emptyCount++
                } else if (v != solution[r][c]) {
                    solutionMismatches++
                }
            }
        }

        if (emptyCount > 0) {
            return WinConditionResult(
                isWon = false,
                isComplete = false,
                hasConflicts = false,
                emptyCellsCount = emptyCount,
                message = "$emptyCount cells remain empty"
            )
        }

        val isStandardValid = isBoardCompleteAndValid(currentBoard)
        if (!isStandardValid || solutionMismatches > 0) {
            return WinConditionResult(
                isWon = false,
                isComplete = true,
                hasConflicts = true,
                emptyCellsCount = 0,
                message = "Board is full but contains invalid placements"
            )
        }

        return WinConditionResult(
            isWon = true,
            isComplete = true,
            hasConflicts = false,
            emptyCellsCount = 0,
            message = "Victory! Sudoku puzzle solved perfectly!"
        )
    }

    /**
     * Simple boolean check for whether the board matches the win condition.
     */
    fun isWin(currentBoard: Array<IntArray>, solution: Array<IntArray>): Boolean {
        return checkWinCondition(currentBoard, solution).isWon
    }

    fun isBoardCompleteAndValid(board: Array<IntArray>): Boolean {
        for (r in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
                val v = board[r][c]
                if (v !in 1..9) return false
                board[r][c] = 0
                val safe = isSafe(board, r, c, v)
                board[r][c] = v
                if (!safe) return false
            }
        }
        return true
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. HINTS & CANDIDATES
    // -------------------------------------------------------------

    fun findHint(currentBoard: Array<IntArray>, solution: Array<IntArray>): HintResult? {
        val emptyCells = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, Int>>()
        for (r in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
            for (c in 0 until GRID_SIZE) {
                if (currentBoard[r][c] == 0) {
                    emptyCells.add(Pair(r, c))
                }
            }
        }
        if (emptyCells.isEmpty()) return null
        val (hr, hc) = emptyCells.random()
        return HintResult(hr, hc, solution[hr][hc])
    }

    fun getCandidates(board: Array<IntArray>, row: Int, col: Int): Set<Int> {
        if (board[row][col] != 0) return emptySet()
        return (1..9).filter { isSafe(board, row, col, it) }.toSet()
    }
}


########################################################################################
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/logic/SudokuScoring.kt
########################################################################################
package com.example.logic

/**
 * Detailed scoring breakdown for a completed or attempted Sudoku match.
 */
data class ScoreResult(
    val basePoints: Int,
    val speedBonus: Int,
    val accuracyBonus: Int,
    val streakBonus: Int,
    val totalEarned: Int
)

/**
 * Transparent, rewarding Sudoku scoring engine.
 *
 * Scoring Formula:
 * - Base points awarded by puzzle difficulty (Easy: 600, Medium: 1200, Hard: 2000, Expert: 3200, Daily: 2400)
 * - Speed bonus for fast solves under difficulty benchmark (up to 800 bonus points)
 * - Accuracy bonus for low mistakes (0 mistakes / Flawless: +500 pts, 1 mistake: +250 pts, 2 mistakes: +100 pts)
 * - Streak multiplier bonus (+75 pts per active day of streak, capped at 750 pts)
 * - Partial completion / effort points (+100 pts) even on game over, ensuring every match updates score!
 */
object SudokuScoring {

    fun calculateScore(
        difficulty: Difficulty,
        timeSeconds: Long,
        mistakes: Int,
        streak: Int,
        isWon: Boolean
    ): ScoreResult {
        if (!isWon) {
            return ScoreResult(
                basePoints = 100,
                speedBonus = 0,
                accuracyBonus = 0,
                streakBonus = 0,
                totalEarned = 100
            )
        }

        val base = when (difficulty) {
            Difficulty.EASY -> 600
            Difficulty.MEDIUM -> 1200
            Difficulty.HARD -> 2000
            Difficulty.EXPERT -> 3200
            Difficulty.DAILY -> 2400
        }

        val benchmarkSeconds = when (difficulty) {
            Difficulty.EASY -> 420L   // 7 mins
            Difficulty.MEDIUM -> 720L // 12 mins
            Difficulty.HARD -> 1200L  // 20 mins
            Difficulty.EXPERT -> 1800L// 30 mins
            Difficulty.DAILY -> 900L  // 15 mins
        }

        val speedBonus = if (timeSeconds < benchmarkSeconds) {
            ((benchmarkSeconds - timeSeconds) * 2).coerceIn(0L, 800L).toInt()
        } else {
            0
        }

        val accuracyBonus = when (mistakes) {
            0 -> 500 // Flawless clean sweep
            1 -> 250
            2 -> 100
            else -> 0
        }

        val streakBonus = (streak.coerceAtLeast(1) * 75).coerceAtMost(750)
        val total = base + speedBonus + accuracyBonus + streakBonus

        return ScoreResult(
            basePoints = base,
            speedBonus = speedBonus,
            accuracyBonus = accuracyBonus,
            streakBonus = streakBonus,
            totalEarned = total
        )
    }
}

########################################################################################
FILE: firestore.rules
########################################################################################
rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isOwner(userId) {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;
    }

    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() >= 1 && id.size() <= 128 && id.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$');
    }

    function incoming() {
      return request.resource.data;
    }

    function existing() {
      return resource.data;
    }

    function isValidGameSession(data) {
      return data.userId is string && data.userId.size() >= 1 && data.userId.size() <= 128 &&
             data.userId == request.auth.uid &&
             data.difficulty is string && data.difficulty in ['EASY', 'MEDIUM', 'HARD', 'EXPERT', 'DAILY'] &&
             data.timerSeconds is int && data.timerSeconds >= 0 && data.timerSeconds <= 3600000 &&
             data.mistakes is int && data.mistakes >= 0 && data.mistakes <= 100 &&
             data.isPaused is bool &&
             data.isDaily is bool &&
             data.boardData is string && data.boardData.size() >= 1 && data.boardData.size() <= 2000 &&
             data.solutionData is string && data.solutionData.size() >= 1 && data.solutionData.size() <= 500 &&
             data.notesData is string && data.notesData.size() <= 4000 &&
             data.createdAt is timestamp && data.createdAt <= request.time &&
             data.updatedAt is timestamp && data.updatedAt <= request.time;
    }

    function isValidDailyChallenge(data) {
      return data.userId is string && data.userId.size() >= 1 && data.userId.size() <= 128 &&
             data.userId == request.auth.uid &&
             data.dateString is string && data.dateString.size() >= 8 && data.dateString.size() <= 20 &&
             data.difficulty is string && data.difficulty in ['EASY', 'MEDIUM', 'HARD', 'EXPERT', 'DAILY'] &&
             data.completed is bool &&
             data.timeSpentSeconds is int && data.timeSpentSeconds >= 0 && data.timeSpentSeconds <= 3600000 &&
             data.mistakes is int && data.mistakes >= 0 && data.mistakes <= 100 &&
             data.starsAwarded is int && data.starsAwarded >= 0 && data.starsAwarded <= 5 &&
             data.createdAt is timestamp && data.createdAt <= request.time &&
             data.updatedAt is timestamp && data.updatedAt <= request.time;
    }

    function isValidSticker(data) {
      return data.userId is string && data.userId.size() >= 1 && data.userId.size() <= 128 &&
             data.userId == request.auth.uid &&
             data.title is string && data.title.size() >= 1 && data.title.size() <= 100 &&
             data.description is string && data.description.size() >= 1 && data.description.size() <= 300 &&
             data.iconName is string && data.iconName.size() >= 1 && data.iconName.size() <= 50 &&
             data.category is string && data.category.size() >= 1 && data.category.size() <= 50 &&
             data.unlocked is bool &&
             data.createdAt is timestamp && data.createdAt <= request.time &&
             data.updatedAt is timestamp && data.updatedAt <= request.time;
    }

    function isValidGameStats(data) {
      return data.userId is string && data.userId.size() >= 1 && data.userId.size() <= 128 &&
             data.userId == request.auth.uid &&
             data.difficulty is string && data.difficulty in ['EASY', 'MEDIUM', 'HARD', 'EXPERT', 'DAILY'] &&
             data.gamesPlayed is int && data.gamesPlayed >= 0 && data.gamesPlayed <= 1000000 &&
             data.gamesWon is int && data.gamesWon >= 0 && data.gamesWon <= data.gamesPlayed &&
             data.bestTimeSeconds is int && data.bestTimeSeconds >= 0 && data.bestTimeSeconds <= 3600000 &&
             data.totalTimeSeconds is int && data.totalTimeSeconds >= 0 && data.totalTimeSeconds <= 360000000 &&
             data.winStreak is int && data.winStreak >= 0 && data.winStreak <= 1000000 &&
             data.perfectGames is int && data.perfectGames >= 0 && data.perfectGames <= data.gamesWon &&
             data.createdAt is timestamp && data.createdAt <= request.time &&
             data.updatedAt is timestamp && data.updatedAt <= request.time;
    }

    function isValidLeaderboard(data) {
      return data.userId is string && data.userId.size() >= 1 && data.userId.size() <= 128 &&
             data.userId == request.auth.uid &&
             data.username is string && data.username.size() >= 1 && data.username.size() <= 30 &&
             data.score is int && data.score >= 0 && data.score <= 10000000 &&
             data.gamesWon is int && data.gamesWon >= 0 && data.gamesWon <= 1000000 &&
             data.winStreak is int && data.winStreak >= 0 && data.winStreak <= 1000000 &&
             data.bestTimeSeconds is int && data.bestTimeSeconds >= 0 && data.bestTimeSeconds <= 3600000 &&
             data.bestTime is string && data.bestTime.size() <= 30 &&
             data.title is string && data.title.size() >= 1 && data.title.size() <= 50 &&
             data.updatedAt is timestamp && data.updatedAt <= request.time;
    }

    match /leaderboard/{userId} {
      allow get, list: if isSignedIn();
      allow create: if isOwner(userId) &&
                       isValidId(userId) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasAll([
                         'userId', 'username', 'score', 'gamesWon', 'winStreak',
                         'bestTimeSeconds', 'bestTime', 'title', 'updatedAt'
                       ]) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                         'userId', 'username', 'score', 'gamesWon', 'winStreak',
                         'bestTimeSeconds', 'bestTime', 'title', 'updatedAt'
                       ]) &&
                       isValidLeaderboard(incoming());

      allow update: if isOwner(userId) &&
                       isValidId(userId) &&
                       isValidLeaderboard(incoming()) &&
                       incoming().userId == existing().userId &&
                       incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
                         'username', 'score', 'gamesWon', 'winStreak',
                         'bestTimeSeconds', 'bestTime', 'title', 'updatedAt'
                       ]);

      allow delete: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId);
    }

    match /users/{userId} {
      match /sessions/{sessionId} {
        allow get: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId) && isValidId(sessionId);
        allow list: if isOwner(userId) && existing().userId == request.auth.uid;

        allow create: if isOwner(userId) &&
                         isValidId(userId) &&
                         isValidId(sessionId) &&
                         incoming().keys().hasAll([
                           'userId', 'difficulty', 'timerSeconds', 'mistakes',
                           'isPaused', 'isDaily', 'boardData', 'solutionData',
                           'notesData', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                         ]) &&
                         incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                           'userId', 'difficulty', 'timerSeconds', 'mistakes',
                           'isPaused', 'isDaily', 'boardData', 'solutionData',
                           'notesData', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                         ]) &&
                         isValidGameSession(incoming());

        allow update: if isOwner(userId) &&
                         isValidId(userId) &&
                         isValidId(sessionId) &&
                         isValidGameSession(incoming()) &&
                         incoming().userId == existing().userId &&
                         incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt &&
                         incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
                           'difficulty', 'timerSeconds', 'mistakes',
                           'isPaused', 'isDaily', 'boardData', 'solutionData',
                           'notesData', 'updatedAt'
                         ]);

        allow delete: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId) && isValidId(sessionId);
      }

      match /daily_challenges/{dateString} {
        allow get: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId) && isValidId(dateString);
        allow list: if isOwner(userId) && existing().userId == request.auth.uid;

        allow create: if isOwner(userId) &&
                         isValidId(userId) &&
                         isValidId(dateString) &&
                         incoming().dateString == dateString &&
                         incoming().keys().hasAll([
                           'userId', 'dateString', 'difficulty', 'completed',
                           'timeSpentSeconds', 'mistakes', 'starsAwarded',
                           'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                         ]) &&
                         incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                           'userId', 'dateString', 'difficulty', 'completed',
                           'timeSpentSeconds', 'mistakes', 'starsAwarded',
                           'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                         ]) &&
                         isValidDailyChallenge(incoming());

        allow update: if isOwner(userId) &&
                         isValidId(userId) &&
                         isValidId(dateString) &&
                         isValidDailyChallenge(incoming()) &&
                         incoming().userId == existing().userId &&
                         incoming().dateString == existing().dateString &&
                         incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt &&
                         incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
                           'difficulty', 'completed', 'timeSpentSeconds',
                           'mistakes', 'starsAwarded', 'updatedAt'
                         ]);

        allow delete: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId) && isValidId(dateString);
      }

      match /stickers/{stickerId} {
        allow get: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId) && isValidId(stickerId);
        allow list: if isOwner(userId) && existing().userId == request.auth.uid;

        allow create: if isOwner(userId) &&
                         isValidId(userId) &&
                         isValidId(stickerId) &&
                         incoming().keys().hasAll([
                           'userId', 'title', 'description', 'iconName',
                           'category', 'unlocked', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                         ]) &&
                         incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                           'userId', 'title', 'description', 'iconName',
                           'category', 'unlocked', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                         ]) &&
                         isValidSticker(incoming());

        allow update: if isOwner(userId) &&
                         isValidId(userId) &&
                         isValidId(stickerId) &&
                         isValidSticker(incoming()) &&
                         incoming().userId == existing().userId &&
                         incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt &&
                         incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
                           'title', 'description', 'iconName', 'category',
                           'unlocked', 'updatedAt'
                         ]);

        allow delete: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId) && isValidId(stickerId);
      }

      match /game_stats/{difficulty} {
        allow get: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId) && isValidId(difficulty);
        allow list: if isOwner(userId) && existing().userId == request.auth.uid;

        allow create: if isOwner(userId) &&
                         isValidId(userId) &&
                         isValidId(difficulty) &&
                         incoming().difficulty == difficulty &&
                         incoming().keys().hasAll([
                           'userId', 'difficulty', 'gamesPlayed', 'gamesWon',
                           'bestTimeSeconds', 'totalTimeSeconds', 'winStreak',
                           'perfectGames', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                         ]) &&
                         incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                           'userId', 'difficulty', 'gamesPlayed', 'gamesWon',
                           'bestTimeSeconds', 'totalTimeSeconds', 'winStreak',
                           'perfectGames', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                         ]) &&
                         isValidGameStats(incoming());

        allow update: if isOwner(userId) &&
                         isValidId(userId) &&
                         isValidId(difficulty) &&
                         isValidGameStats(incoming()) &&
                         incoming().userId == existing().userId &&
                         incoming().difficulty == existing().difficulty &&
                         incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt &&
                         incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
                           'gamesPlayed', 'gamesWon', 'bestTimeSeconds',
                           'totalTimeSeconds', 'winStreak', 'perfectGames',
                           'updatedAt'
                         ]);

        allow delete: if isOwner(userId) && isValidId(userId) && isValidId(difficulty);
      }
    }
  }
}

########################################################################################
FILE: UI_UPGRADE_NOTES.md
########################################################################################
# Obsidian Sudoku — UI & motion upgrade

Scope: the Play experience. No game logic, ViewModel, data or Firebase code was changed.

## New
- `ui/components/Fx.kt` — `Modifier.bounceClick` (spring press-scale + haptic tick) and `ConfettiBurst` (Canvas particles).

## Rewritten
- `SudokuGridView.kt`
  - Board intro ripple from the top-left whenever a new puzzle starts
  - Animated selection ring that trails out as you move
  - Digit spring-pop when placed; wrong digit shakes the cell + haptic
  - Completion wave when a row, column or box is finished
  - Crisp Canvas grid lines with glowing 3x3 block lines, gradient frame, ambient glow
  - Digit and note sizes scale to the cell; notes matching the selected number highlight
  - TalkBack: every cell announces row, column, value / notes, given, incorrect
- `SudokuControls.kt` — equal-width action buttons, taller numpad (64dp), press bounce, per-digit progress meter,
  highlight of the selected number, notes-mode tint, fully theme-aware
- `PersistentTimerComponent.kt`, `SudokuPauseOverlay.kt`, `DifficultyDialog.kt` — theme-aware, animated
- `PlayScreen.kt` — mistake pips with shake, animated feedback banner (fade/expand), tablet width cap,
  pause overlay now covers the board exactly, victory screen (confetti, count-up score, animated stars),
  game-over screen (spring-in, wobble)

## Not yet built or run
This was written without an Android SDK, so it has not been compiled. Open in Android Studio and run
`./gradlew assembleDebug`. If a compile error appears, it is most likely a missing import.


########################################################################################
FILE: firestore.test.js
########################################################################################
const {
  initializeTestEnvironment,
  assertFails,
  assertSucceeds,
} = require("@firebase/rules-unit-testing");
const { test, before, after, beforeEach } = require("node:test");
const fs = require("node:fs");

let testEnv;
const PROJECT_ID = process.env.GCP_PROJECT || "demo-no-project";
const ALICE_UID = "alice_123";
const BOB_UID = "bob_456";

const [emulatorHost, emulatorPortStr] = (
  process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST || "127.0.0.1:8085"
).split(":");
const emulatorPort = parseInt(emulatorPortStr, 10);

const nowTimestamp = () => new Date(Date.now() - 1000);

before(async () => {
  const rules = fs.readFileSync("./firestore.rules", "utf8");
  testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
    projectId: PROJECT_ID,
    firestore: {
      rules,
      host: emulatorHost,
      port: emulatorPort,
    },
  });
});

after(async () => {
  if (testEnv) {
    await testEnv.cleanup();
  }
});

beforeEach(async () => {
  if (testEnv) {
    await testEnv.clearFirestore();
  }
});

// --- SECURITY BOUNDS TESTS (Dirty Dozen) ---

test("1. Unauthenticated user: cannot read or list sessions", async () => {
  const unauthDb = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();
  await assertFails(
    unauthDb
      .collection("users")
      .doc(ALICE_UID)
      .collection("sessions")
      .doc("active")
      .get()
  );
  await assertFails(
    unauthDb
      .collection("users")
      .doc(ALICE_UID)
      .collection("sessions")
      .where("userId", "==", ALICE_UID)
      .get()
  );
});

test("2. Cross-user read: Bob cannot read Alice's sessions, stickers, daily_challenges, or game_stats", async () => {
  const now = nowTimestamp();
  await testEnv.withSecurityRulesDisabled(async (context) => {
    const db = context.firestore();
    await db
      .collection("users")
      .doc(ALICE_UID)
      .collection("sessions")
      .doc("active")
      .set({
        userId: ALICE_UID,
        difficulty: "EASY",
        timerSeconds: 45,
        mistakes: 1,
        isPaused: false,
        isDaily: false,
        boardData: "0:0:0",
        solutionData: "1",
        notesData: "",
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      });
  });

  const bobDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(BOB_UID).firestore();
  await assertFails(
    bobDb
      .collection("users")
      .doc(ALICE_UID)
      .collection("sessions")
      .doc("active")
      .get()
  );
  await assertFails(
    bobDb
      .collection("users")
      .doc(ALICE_UID)
      .collection("sessions")
      .where("userId", "==", ALICE_UID)
      .get()
  );
});

test("3. Cross-user write: Bob cannot create or update in Alice's subcollections", async () => {
  const bobDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(BOB_UID).firestore();
  await assertFails(
    bobDb
      .collection("users")
      .doc(ALICE_UID)
      .collection("sessions")
      .doc("active")
      .set({
        userId: ALICE_UID,
        difficulty: "EASY",
        timerSeconds: 10,
        mistakes: 0,
        isPaused: false,
        isDaily: false,
        boardData: "0:0:0",
        solutionData: "1",
        notesData: "",
        createdAt: nowTimestamp(),
        updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
      })
  );
});

test("4. Identity spoofing in payload: Alice cannot write userId = Bob in her own subcollection", async () => {
  const aliceDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(ALICE_UID).firestore();
  await assertFails(
    aliceDb
      .collection("users")
      .doc(ALICE_UID)
      .collection("sessions")
      .doc("active")
      .set({
        userId: BOB_UID,
        difficulty: "EASY",
        timerSeconds: 10,
        mistakes: 0,
        isPaused: false,
        isDaily: false,
        boardData: "0:0:0",
        solutionData: "1",
        notesData: "",
        createdAt: nowTimestamp(),
        updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
      })
  );
});

test("5. Shadow update (ghost field): Alice cannot inject extra fields on create or update", async () => {
  const aliceDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(ALICE_UID).firestore();
  const docRef = aliceDb
    .collection("users")
    .doc(ALICE_UID)
    .collection("game_stats")
    .doc("EASY");

  await assertFails(
    docRef.set({
      userId: ALICE_UID,
      difficulty: "EASY",
      gamesPlayed: 1,
      gamesWon: 1,
      bestTimeSeconds: 120,
      totalTimeSeconds: 120,
      winStreak: 1,
      perfectGames: 1,
      isAdmin: true,
      createdAt: nowTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );
});

test("6. Authenticated owner: can create, read, list, update, and delete valid GameSession, DailyChallenge, Sticker, and GameStats", async () => {
  const aliceDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(ALICE_UID).firestore();

  // GameSession create & get
  const sessionRef = aliceDb
    .collection("users")
    .doc(ALICE_UID)
    .collection("sessions")
    .doc("active");
  await assertSucceeds(
    sessionRef.set({
      userId: ALICE_UID,
      difficulty: "EASY",
      timerSeconds: 30,
      mistakes: 0,
      isPaused: false,
      isDaily: false,
      boardData: "1:1:0,0:0:0",
      solutionData: "1,2",
      notesData: ";",
      createdAt: nowTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(sessionRef.get());
  await assertSucceeds(
    sessionRef.update({
      timerSeconds: 35,
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );

  // DailyChallenge create & list with userId filter
  const dailyRef = aliceDb
    .collection("users")
    .doc(ALICE_UID)
    .collection("daily_challenges")
    .doc("2026-10-17");
  await assertSucceeds(
    dailyRef.set({
      userId: ALICE_UID,
      dateString: "2026-10-17",
      difficulty: "DAILY",
      completed: true,
      timeSpentSeconds: 180,
      mistakes: 0,
      starsAwarded: 3,
      createdAt: nowTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(
    aliceDb
      .collection("users")
      .doc(ALICE_UID)
      .collection("daily_challenges")
      .where("userId", "==", ALICE_UID)
      .get()
  );

  // Sticker create & update
  const stickerRef = aliceDb
    .collection("users")
    .doc(ALICE_UID)
    .collection("stickers")
    .doc("first_win");
  await assertSucceeds(
    stickerRef.set({
      userId: ALICE_UID,
      title: "Obsidian Initiate",
      description: "Complete your first Sudoku puzzle",
      iconName: "sparkles",
      category: "Milestone",
      unlocked: false,
      createdAt: nowTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(
    stickerRef.update({
      unlocked: true,
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );

  // GameStats create & update
  const statsRef = aliceDb
    .collection("users")
    .doc(ALICE_UID)
    .collection("game_stats")
    .doc("EASY");
  await assertSucceeds(
    statsRef.set({
      userId: ALICE_UID,
      difficulty: "EASY",
      gamesPlayed: 1,
      gamesWon: 1,
      bestTimeSeconds: 150,
      totalTimeSeconds: 150,
      winStreak: 1,
      perfectGames: 1,
      createdAt: nowTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(
    statsRef.update({
      gamesPlayed: 2,
      gamesWon: 2,
      winStreak: 2,
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );
});

test("7. Realtime Leaderboard: authenticated users can read, owner can write, cross-user write denied", async () => {
  const unauthDb = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();
  await assertFails(unauthDb.collection("leaderboard").doc(ALICE_UID).get());

  const aliceDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(ALICE_UID).firestore();
  const aliceLeadRef = aliceDb.collection("leaderboard").doc(ALICE_UID);
  await assertSucceeds(
    aliceLeadRef.set({
      userId: ALICE_UID,
      username: "AliceSolver",
      score: 1500,
      gamesWon: 10,
      winStreak: 5,
      bestTimeSeconds: 120,
      bestTime: "02:00",
      title: "Obsidian Solver",
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );

  // Bob can read Alice's public entry
  const bobDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(BOB_UID).firestore();
  await assertSucceeds(bobDb.collection("leaderboard").doc(ALICE_UID).get());
  await assertSucceeds(bobDb.collection("leaderboard").get());

  // Bob cannot modify Alice's entry
  await assertFails(
    bobDb.collection("leaderboard").doc(ALICE_UID).update({
      score: 9999,
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );

  // Alice can update her username or score
  await assertSucceeds(
    aliceLeadRef.update({
      username: "AliceGrandmaster",
      score: 2000,
      updatedAt: nowTimestamp(),
    })
  );
});
